00641:     def _confirmation_replay_response(
00642:         self,
00643:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
00644:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
00645:         cached = conversation_payload.get(
00646:             "lastSuccessfulAction"
00647:         )
00648: 
00649:         if not isinstance(cached, dict):
00650:             return None
00651: 
00652:         try:
00653:             completed_at = float(
00654:                 cached.get("completedAtEpoch")
00655:             )
00656:         except (TypeError, ValueError):
00657:             return None
00658: 
00659:         age_seconds = time.time() - completed_at
00660: 
00661:         if (
00662:             age_seconds < 0
00663:             or age_seconds
00664:             > CONFIRM_REPLAY_TTL_SECONDS
00665:         ):
00666:             return None
00667: 
00668:         action_id = str(
00669:             cached.get("actionId") or ""
00670:         ).strip()
00671: 
00672:         action_type = str(
00673:             cached.get("actionType") or ""
00674:         ).strip()
00675: 
00676:         if not action_id or not action_type:
00677:             return None
00678: 
00679:         status = str(
00680:             cached.get("status") or "succeeded"
00681:         ).strip()
00682: 
00683:         reply = str(
00684:             cached.get("reply")
00685:             or self._action_success_reply(
00686:                 action_type
00687:             )
00688:         )
00689: 
00690:         return {
00691:             "reply": reply,
00692:             "requiresConfirmation": False,
00693:             "action": {
00694:                 "id": action_id,
00695:                 "type": action_type,
00696:                 "status": status,
00697:             },
00698:             "data": cached.get("data"),
00699:         }
00700: 
00701:     async def _wait_for_confirmation_replay(
00702:         self,
00703:         context: AgentContext,
00704:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
00705:         interval_seconds = 0.2
00706:         attempts = max(
00707:             1,
00708:             int(
00709:                 CONFIRM_REPLAY_WAIT_SECONDS
00710:                 / interval_seconds
00711:             ),
00712:         )
00713: 
00714:         for attempt in range(attempts):
00715:             current_payload = (
00716:                 await self._load_conversation_payload(
00717:                     context
00718:                 )
00719:             )
00720: 
00721:             replay_response = (
00722:                 self._confirmation_replay_response(
00723:                     current_payload
00724:                 )
00725:             )
00726: 
00727:             if replay_response is not None:
00728:                 return replay_response
00729: 
00730:             if attempt + 1 < attempts:
00731:                 await asyncio.sleep(
00732:                     interval_seconds
00733:                 )
00734: 
00735:         return None
00736: 
00737:     async def _handle_pending_action_reply(
00738:         self,
00739:         context: AgentContext,
00740:         text: str,
00741:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
00742:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
00743:         reply_kind = (
00744:             self._pending_action_reply_kind(
00745:                 text
00746:             )
00747:         )
00748: 
00749:         if reply_kind is None:
00750:             return None
00751: 
00752:         employee_id = str(
00753:             context.employee_id or ""
00754:         ).strip()
00755: 
00756:         if not employee_id:
00757:             raise AppError(
00758:                 "کد پرسنلی کاربر مشخص نیست.",
00759:                 code="MISSING_EMPLOYEE_ID",
00760:             )
00761: 
00762:         pending_action = None
00763:         pending_action_id = str(
00764:             conversation_payload.get(
00765:                 "pendingActionId"
00766:             )
00767:             or ""
00768:         ).strip()
00769: 
00770:         if pending_action_id:
00771:             pending_action = (
00772:                 await asyncio.to_thread(
00773:                     self.repository.get_pending_action,
00774:                     pending_action_id,
00775:                     employee_id,
00776:                 )
00777:             )
00778: 
00779:             if (
00780:                 pending_action is not None
00781:                 and pending_action.status
00782:                 != "pending"
00783:             ):
00784:                 pending_action = None
00785: 
00786:         if (
00787:             pending_action is None
00788:             and not str(
00789:                 context.conversation_id or ""
00790:             ).strip()
00791:         ):
00792:             pending_action = (
00793:                 await asyncio.to_thread(
00794:                     self.repository
00795:                     .get_latest_pending_action,
00796:                     employee_id,
00797:                 )
00798:             )
00799: 
00800:         if reply_kind == "confirm":
00801:             if pending_action is None:
00802:                 replay_response = (
00803:                     self._confirmation_replay_response(
00804:                         conversation_payload
00805:                     )
00806:                 )
00807: 
00808:                 if (
00809:                     replay_response is None
00810:                     and pending_action_id
00811:                 ):
00812:                     replay_response = (
00813:                         await self
00814:                         ._wait_for_confirmation_replay(
00815:                             context
00816:                         )
00817:                     )
00818: 
00819:                 if replay_response is not None:
00820:                     return replay_response
00821: 
00822:                 return {
00823:                     "reply": (
00824:                         "در این مکالمه عملیاتی "
00825:                         "در انتظار تأیید نیست."
00826:                     ),
00827:                     "requiresConfirmation": False,
00828:                     "data": {
00829:                         "pendingAction": None,
00830:                     },
00831:                 }
00832: 
00833:             try:
00834:                 result = await self.actions.confirm(
00835:                     context,
00836:                     pending_action.id,
00837:                 )
00838:             except AppError as exc:
00839:                 details = (
00840:                     getattr(exc, "details", None)
00841:                     or {}
00842:                 )
00843: 
00844:                 current_status = str(
00845:                     details.get("status") or ""
00846:                 ).strip()
00847: 
00848:                 if (
00849:                     getattr(
00850:                         exc,
00851:                         "status_code",
00852:                         None,
00853:                     )
00854:                     == 409
00855:                     and current_status
00856:                     in {
00857:                         "processing",
00858:                         "succeeded",
00859:                     }
00860:                 ):
00861:                     replay_response = (
00862:                         await self
00863:                         ._wait_for_confirmation_replay(
00864:                             context
00865:                         )
00866:                     )
00867: 
00868:                     if replay_response is not None:
00869:                         return replay_response
00870: 
00871:                 raise
00872: 
00873:             success_reply = (
00874:                 self._action_success_reply(
00875:                     pending_action.action_type
00876:                 )
00877:             )
00878: 
00879:             success_status = str(
00880:                 result.get("status")
00881:                 or "succeeded"
00882:             )
00883: 
00884:             success_data = result.get(
00885:                 "result",
00886:                 result,
00887:             )
00888: 
00889:             await self._save_conversation_payload(
00890:                 context,
00891:                 {
00892:                     "lastSuccessfulAction": {
00893:                         "actionId": (
00894:                             pending_action.id
00895:                         ),
00896:                         "actionType": (
00897:                             pending_action.action_type
00898:                         ),
00899:                         "status": success_status,
00900:                         "reply": success_reply,
00901:                         "data": success_data,
00902:                         "completedAtEpoch": (
00903:                             time.time()
00904:                         ),
00905:                     },
00906:                 },
00907:             )
00908: 
00909:             return {
00910:                 "reply": success_reply,
00911:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
00912:                 "action": {
00913:                     "id": pending_action.id,
00914:                     "type": (
00915:                         pending_action.action_type
00916:                     ),
00917:                     "status": success_status,
00918:                 },
00919:                 "data": success_data,
00920:             }
00921: 
00922:         if reply_kind == "reject":
00923:             if pending_action is not None:
00924:                 await self.actions.cancel(
00925:                     context,
00926:                     pending_action.id,
00927:                 )
00928: 
00929:             if not conversation_payload:
00930:                 return {
00931:                     "reply": (
00932:                         "پیشنهاد فعالی برای رد کردن "
00933:                         "در این مکالمه وجود ندارد."
00934:                     ),
00935:                     "requiresConfirmation": False,
00936:                 }
00937: 
00938:             updated_payload = dict(
00939:                 conversation_payload
00940:             )
00941:             updated_payload.pop(
00942:                 "pendingActionId",
00943:                 None,
00944:             )
00945:             proposal = (
00946:                 updated_payload.get("proposal")
00947:                 if isinstance(
00948:                     updated_payload.get(
00949:                         "proposal"
00950:                     ),
00951:                     dict,
00952:                 )
00953:                 else {}
00954:             )
00955:             updated_payload[
00956:                 "proposalRejected"
00957:             ] = True
00958: 
00959:             nlu_state = (
00960:                 dict(
00961:                     updated_payload.get(
00962:                         "nluState"
00963:                     )
00964:                     or {}
00965:                 )
00966:             )
00967:             nlu_state[
00968:                 "proposalRejected"
00969:             ] = True
00970:             nlu_state[
00971:                 "missingFields"
00972:             ] = ["alternative"]
00973:             updated_payload[
00974:                 "nluState"
00975:             ] = nlu_state
00976: 
00977:             await self._save_conversation_payload(
00978:                 context,
00979:                 updated_payload,
00980:             )
00981: 
00982:             return {
00983:                 "reply": self._rejected_proposal_reply(
00984:                     proposal,
00985:                     updated_payload,
00986:                 ),
00987:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
00988:                 "data": {
00989:                     "proposalRejected": True,
00990:                 },
00991:             }
00992: 
00993:         if pending_action is not None:
00994:             result = await self.actions.cancel(
00995:                 context,
00996:                 pending_action.id,
00997:             )
00998:             message = (
00999:                 result.get("message")
01000:                 or "عملیات لغو شد."
01001:             )
01002:         else:
01003:             message = (
01004:                 "فرایند فعال این مکالمه لغو شد."
01005:             )
01006: 
01007:         await self._delete_conversation_state(
01008:             context
01009:         )
01010: 
01011:         return {
01012:             "reply": message,
01013:             "requiresConfirmation": False,
01014:             "data": {
01015:                 "conversationCancelled": True,
01016:             },
01017:         }
01018: 
01019:     async def _load_conversation_payload(
01020:         self,
01021:         context: AgentContext,
01022:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
01023:         employee_id = str(
01024:             context.employee_id or ""
01025:         ).strip()
01026: 
01027:         if not employee_id:
01028:             return {}
01029: 
01030:         state = await asyncio.to_thread(
01031:             self.repository.get_chat_state,
01032:             employee_id,
01033:             context.conversation_id,
01034:         )
01035: 
01036:         if not state:
01037:             return {}
01038: 
01039:         if str(
01040:             state.get("stateType") or ""
01041:         ) != CONVERSATION_STATE:
01042:             return {}
01043: 
01044:         payload = state.get("payload")
01045: 
01046:         return (
01047:             dict(payload)
01048:             if isinstance(payload, dict)
01049:             else {}
01050:         )
01051: 
01052:     async def _save_conversation_payload(
01053:         self,
01054:         context: AgentContext,
01055:         payload: dict[str, Any],
01056:     ) -> None:
01057:         employee_id = str(
01058:             context.employee_id or ""
01059:         ).strip()
01060: 
01061:         if not employee_id:
01062:             return
01063: 
01064:         await asyncio.to_thread(
01065:             self.repository.save_chat_state,
01066:             employee_id,
01067:             CONVERSATION_STATE,
01068:             payload,
01069:             CONVERSATION_STATE_TTL_MINUTES,
01070:             context.conversation_id,
01071:         )
01072: 
01073:     async def _delete_conversation_state(
01074:         self,
01075:         context: AgentContext,
01076:     ) -> None:
01077:         employee_id = str(
01078:             context.employee_id or ""
01079:         ).strip()
01080: 
01081:         if not employee_id:
01082:             return
01083: 
01084:         await asyncio.to_thread(
01085:             self.repository.delete_chat_state,
01086:             employee_id,
01087:             context.conversation_id,
01088:         )

01136:     @staticmethod
01137:     def _nlu_state_with_active_date(
01138:         previous_state: dict[str, Any] | None,
01139:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01140:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
01141:         if not previous_state:
01142:             return previous_state
01143: 
01144:         active_date = str(
01145:             conversation_payload.get(
01146:                 "activeDate"
01147:             )
01148:             or ""
01149:         ).strip()
01150: 
01151:         if not active_date:
01152:             return previous_state
01153: 
01154:         updated_state = dict(
01155:             previous_state
01156:         )
01157:         entities = (
01158:             dict(
01159:                 updated_state.get(
01160:                     "entities"
01161:                 )
01162:                 or {}
01163:             )
01164:         )
01165: 
01166:         if (
01167:             updated_state.get("domain")
01168:             == "FOOD"
01169:             and not entities.get("date")
01170:         ):
01171:             entities["date"] = active_date
01172:             updated_state["entities"] = (
01173:                 entities
01174:             )
01175: 
01176:         return updated_state

01178:     def _enrich_food_nlu_result(
01179:         self,
01180:         nlu_result: dict[str, Any],
01181:         text: str,
01182:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01183:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
01184:         result = dict(nlu_result)
01185: 
01186:         # FOOD_CONVERSATION_V1
01187:         food_follow_up_candidates = (
01188:             self._find_food_candidates(
01189:                 text,
01190:                 conversation_payload,
01191:             )
01192:         )
01193: 
01194:         candidate_dates = {
01195:             str(
01196:                 item.get(
01197:                     "day",
01198:                     {},
01199:                 ).get("date")
01200:                 or ""
01201:             ).strip()
01202:             for item in food_follow_up_candidates
01203:             if isinstance(
01204:                 item,
01205:                 dict,
01206:             )
01207:             and str(
01208:                 item.get(
01209:                     "day",
01210:                     {},
01211:                 ).get("date")
01212:                 or ""
01213:             ).strip()
01214:         }
01215: 
01216:         unique_candidate_date = (
01217:             next(
01218:                 iter(candidate_dates)
01219:             )
01220:             if len(candidate_dates) == 1
01221:             else None
01222:         )
01223: 
01224:         is_food_follow_up = (
01225:             str(
01226:                 conversation_payload.get(
01227:                     "lastDomain"
01228:                 )
01229:                 or ""
01230:             )
01231:             == "FOOD"
01232:             and bool(
01233:                 conversation_payload.get(
01234:                     "recentMenus"
01235:                 )
01236:             )
01237:             and (
01238:                 bool(
01239:                     self._infer_menu_date(
01240:                         text,
01241:                         conversation_payload,
01242:                     )
01243:                 )
01244:                 or bool(
01245:                     food_follow_up_candidates
01246:                 )
01247:             )
01248:         )
01249: 
01250:         if (
01251:             result.get("domain")
01252:             in {"UNKNOWN", "OUT_OF_SCOPE"}
01253:             and is_food_follow_up
01254:         ):
01255:             inferred_date = (
01256:                 self._infer_menu_date(
01257:                     text,
01258:                     conversation_payload,
01259:                 )
01260:                 or unique_candidate_date
01261:                 or conversation_payload.get(
01262:                     "activeDate"
01263:                 )
01264:             )
01265:             entities: dict[str, Any] = {}
01266: 
01267:             if inferred_date:
01268:                 entities["date"] = str(
01269:                     inferred_date
01270:                 )
01271: 
01272:             missing_fields = (
01273:                 []
01274:                 if inferred_date
01275:                 else ["date"]
01276:             )
01277: 
01278:             result = {
01279:                 "domain": "FOOD",
01280:                 "intent": "RESERVE_FOOD",
01281:                 "entities": entities,
01282:                 "confidence": 0.95,
01283:                 "missingFields": missing_fields,
01284:                 "needsClarification": bool(
01285:                     missing_fields
01286:                 ),
01287:                 "conversationComplete": False,
01288:                 "clarification": (
01289:                     "برای چه روزی غذا رزرو شود؟"
01290:                     if missing_fields
01291:                     else None
01292:                 ),
01293:                 "errors": [],
01294:                 "state": {
01295:                     "domain": "FOOD",
01296:                     "intent": "RESERVE_FOOD",
01297:                     "entities": entities,
01298:                     "missingFields": (
01299:                         missing_fields
01300:                     ),
01301:                 },
01302:             }
01303: 
01304:         if (
01305:             result.get("domain")
01306:             != "FOOD"
01307:             or result.get("intent")
01308:             != "RESERVE_FOOD"
01309:         ):
01310:             return result
01311: 
01312:         entities = dict(
01313:             result.get("entities")
01314:             or {}
01315:         )
01316: 
01317:         inferred_date = (
01318:             entities.get("date")
01319:             or self._infer_menu_date(
01320:                 text,
01321:                 conversation_payload,
01322:             )
01323:             or unique_candidate_date
01324:             or conversation_payload.get(
01325:                 "activeDate"
01326:             )
01327:         )
01328: 
01329:         if inferred_date:
01330:             entities["date"] = str(
01331:                 inferred_date
01332:             )
01333:             result["entities"] = entities
01334: 
01335:             missing_fields = [
01336:                 item
01337:                 for item in (
01338:                     result.get(
01339:                         "missingFields"
01340:                     )
01341:                     or []
01342:                 )
01343:                 if item != "date"
01344:             ]
01345:             result["missingFields"] = (
01346:                 missing_fields
01347:             )
01348:             result["needsClarification"] = (
01349:                 bool(
01350:                     missing_fields
01351:                     or result.get("errors")
01352:                 )
01353:             )
01354: 
01355:             state = dict(
01356:                 result.get("state")
01357:                 or {}
01358:             )
01359:             state["domain"] = "FOOD"
01360:             state["intent"] = (
01361:                 "RESERVE_FOOD"
01362:             )
01363:             state["entities"] = entities
01364:             state["missingFields"] = (
01365:                 missing_fields
01366:             )
01367:             state.pop(
01368:                 "proposalRejected",
01369:                 None,
01370:             )
01371:             result["state"] = state
01372: 
01373:             if not result[
01374:                 "needsClarification"
01375:             ]:
01376:                 result["clarification"] = (
01377:                     None
01378:                 )
01379: 
01380:         return result

01382:     def _combined_conversation_text(
01383:         self,
01384:         text: str,
01385:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01386:         nlu_result: dict[str, Any],
01387:     ) -> str:
01388:         previous_domain = str(
01389:             conversation_payload.get(
01390:                 "lastDomain"
01391:             )
01392:             or ""
01393:         )
01394:         current_domain = str(
01395:             nlu_result.get("domain")
01396:             or ""
01397:         )
01398: 
01399:         previous_text = str(
01400:             conversation_payload.get(
01401:                 "originalMessage"
01402:             )
01403:             or ""
01404:         ).strip()
01405: 
01406:         if (
01407:             previous_domain == "FOOD"
01408:             and current_domain == "FOOD"
01409:             and conversation_payload.get(
01410:                 "proposalRejected"
01411:             )
01412:         ):
01413:             proposal = (
01414:                 conversation_payload.get(
01415:                     "proposal"
01416:                 )
01417:                 if isinstance(
01418:                     conversation_payload.get(
01419:                         "proposal"
01420:                     ),
01421:                     dict,
01422:                 )
01423:                 else {}
01424:             )
01425:             proposal_data = (
01426:                 proposal.get("data")
01427:                 if isinstance(
01428:                     proposal.get("data"),
01429:                     dict,
01430:                 )
01431:                 else {}
01432:             )
01433:             proposal_food = (
01434:                 proposal_data.get("food")
01435:                 if isinstance(
01436:                     proposal_data.get("food"),
01437:                     dict,
01438:                 )
01439:                 else {}
01440:             )
01441:             previous_food_name = str(
01442:                 proposal_food.get(
01443:                     "foodName"
01444:                 )
01445:                 or ""
01446:             ).strip()
01447: 
01448:             current_food_candidates = (
01449:                 self._find_food_candidates(
01450:                     text,
01451:                     conversation_payload,
01452:                 )
01453:             )
01454: 
01455:             if current_food_candidates:
01456:                 return (
01457:                     f"{text} رزرو کن"
01458:                 ).strip()
01459: 
01460:             if (
01461:                 previous_food_name
01462:                 and self._infer_menu_date(
01463:                     text,
01464:                     conversation_payload,
01465:                 )
01466:             ):
01467:                 return (
01468:                     f"{previous_food_name} "
01469:                     f"{text} رزرو کن"
01470:                 ).strip()
01471: 
01472:         previous_nlu_state = (
01473:             conversation_payload.get(
01474:                 "nluState"
01475:             )
01476:             if isinstance(
01477:                 conversation_payload.get(
01478:                     "nluState"
01479:                 ),
01480:                 dict,
01481:             )
01482:             else {}
01483:         )
01484:         previous_intent = str(
01485:             previous_nlu_state.get(
01486:                 "intent"
01487:             )
01488:             or ""
01489:         )
01490:         current_intent = str(
01491:             nlu_result.get("intent")
01492:             or ""
01493:         )
01494: 
01495:         is_explicit_new_request = (
01496:             self._looks_like_new_request(
01497:                 text
01498:             )
01499:             or (
01500:                 current_domain == "FOOD"
01501:                 and bool(
01502:                     self._find_food_candidates(
01503:                         text,
01504:                         conversation_payload,
01505:                     )
01506:                 )
01507:                 and any(
01508:                     keyword in text
01509:                     for keyword in {
01510:                         "رزرو",
01511:                         "بگیر",
01512:                         "سفارش",
01513:                     }
01514:                 )
01515:             )
01516:         )
01517: 
01518:         if (
01519:             previous_text
01520:             and previous_domain
01521:             and previous_domain
01522:             == current_domain
01523:             and previous_intent
01524:             == current_intent
01525:             and not is_explicit_new_request
01526:         ):
01527:             return (
01528:                 f"{previous_text} {text}"
01529:             ).strip()
01530: 
01531:         return text
01532: 
01533:     async def _cancel_pending_on_domain_switch(
01534:         self,
01535:         context: AgentContext,
01536:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01537:         nlu_result: dict[str, Any],
01538:     ) -> None:
01539:         pending_action_id = str(
01540:             conversation_payload.get(
01541:                 "pendingActionId"
01542:             )
01543:             or ""
01544:         ).strip()
01545: 
01546:         if not pending_action_id:
01547:             return
01548: 
01549:         current_domain = str(
01550:             nlu_result.get("domain")
01551:             or ""
01552:         )
01553: 
01554:         if current_domain in {
01555:             "",
01556:             "UNKNOWN",
01557:             "OUT_OF_SCOPE",
01558:         }:
01559:             return
01560: 
01561:         try:
01562:             await self.actions.cancel(
01563:                 context,
01564:                 pending_action_id,
01565:             )
01566:         except AppError:
01567:             pass
01568: 
01569:         conversation_payload.pop(
01570:             "pendingActionId",
01571:             None,
01572:         )
01573:         conversation_payload.pop(
01574:             "proposal",
01575:             None,
01576:         )
01577:         conversation_payload.pop(
01578:             "proposalRejected",
01579:             None,
01580:         )
01581: 
01582:     @staticmethod
01583:     def _dates_from_entities(
01584:         entities: dict[str, Any],
01585:     ) -> list[str]:
01586:         values: list[str] = []
01587: 
01588:         for key in (
01589:             "startDate",
01590:             "endDate",
01591:             "date",
01592:         ):
01593:             value = str(
01594:                 entities.get(key) or ""
01595:             ).strip()
01596: 
01597:             if value and value not in values:
01598:                 values.append(value)
01599: 
01600:         return values
01601: 
01602:     @staticmethod
01603:     def _times_from_entities(
01604:         entities: dict[str, Any],
01605:     ) -> list[str]:
01606:         values: list[str] = []
01607: 
01608:         for key in (
01609:             "startTime",
01610:             "endTime",
01611:             "time",
01612:         ):
01613:             value = str(
01614:                 entities.get(key) or ""
01615:             ).strip()
01616: 
01617:             if value and value not in values:
01618:                 values.append(value)
01619: 
01620:         return values
01621: 
01622:     async def _finalize_response_state(
01623:         self,
01624:         context: AgentContext,
01625:         nlu_result: dict[str, Any],
01626:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01627:         response: dict[str, Any],
01628:         combined_text: str,
01629:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
01630:         response_data = (
01631:             response.get("data")
01632:             if isinstance(
01633:                 response.get("data"),
01634:                 dict,
01635:             )
01636:             else {}
01637:         )
01638: 
01639:         if (
01640:             response_data.get(
01641:                 "awaitingRestaurantConfirmation"
01642:             )
01643:             or response_data.get(
01644:                 "awaitingRestaurantSelection"
01645:             )
01646:         ):
01647:             return response
01648: 
01649:         updated_payload = dict(
01650:             conversation_payload
01651:         )
01652:         updated_payload.update(
01653:             {
01654:                 "nluState": (
01655:                     nlu_result.get("state")
01656:                     or {
01657:                         "domain": (
01658:                             nlu_result.get(
01659:                                 "domain"
01660:                             )
01661:                         ),
01662:                         "intent": (
01663:                             nlu_result.get(
01664:                                 "intent"
01665:                             )
01666:                         ),
01667:                         "entities": (
01668:                             nlu_result.get(
01669:                                 "entities"
01670:                             )
01671:                             or {}
01672:                         ),
01673:                         "missingFields": [],
01674:                     }
01675:                 ),
01676:                 "originalMessage": (
01677:                     combined_text
01678:                 ),
01679:                 "lastDomain": (
01680:                     nlu_result.get("domain")
01681:                 ),
01682:             }
01683:         )
01684: 
01685:         data = response_data
01686: 
01687:         restaurant = data.get(
01688:             "restaurant"
01689:         )
01690: 
01691:         if isinstance(restaurant, dict):
01692:             updated_payload[
01693:                 "restaurant"
01694:             ] = restaurant
01695: 
01696:         days = data.get("days")
01697: 
01698:         if not isinstance(days, list):
01699:             menu_data = (
01700:                 data.get("menu")
01701:                 if isinstance(
01702:                     data.get("menu"),
01703:                     dict,
01704:                 )
01705:                 else {}
01706:             )
01707:             days = menu_data.get("days")
01708: 
01709:         if isinstance(days, list):
01710:             updated_payload[
01711:                 "recentMenus"
01712:             ] = self._merge_menu_days(
01713:                 updated_payload.get(
01714:                     "recentMenus"
01715:                 ),
01716:                 days,
01717:             )
01718: 
01719:             if len(days) == 1:
01720:                 updated_payload[
01721:                     "activeDate"
01722:                 ] = days[0].get(
01723:                     "date"
01724:                 )
01725: 
01726:         pending_action = (
01727:             response.get("pendingAction")
01728:             if isinstance(
01729:                 response.get(
01730:                     "pendingAction"
01731:                 ),
01732:                 dict,
01733:             )
01734:             else {}
01735:         )
01736: 
01737:         pending_action_id = str(
01738:             pending_action.get("id")
01739:             or ""
01740:         ).strip()
01741: 
01742:         if pending_action_id:
01743:             updated_payload[
01744:                 "pendingActionId"
01745:             ] = pending_action_id
01746:             updated_payload[
01747:                 "proposal"
01748:             ] = {
01749:                 "actionType": (
01750:                     nlu_result.get("intent")
01751:                 ),
01752:                 "data": data,
01753:                 "reply": response.get(
01754:                     "reply"
01755:                 ),
01756:             }
01757:             updated_payload.pop(
01758:                 "proposalRejected",
01759:                 None,
01760:             )
01761:         else:
01762:             updated_payload.pop(
01763:                 "pendingActionId",
01764:                 None,
01765:             )
01766: 
01767:         should_preserve = bool(
01768:             pending_action_id
01769:             or updated_payload.get(
01770:                 "recentMenus"
01771:             )
01772:             or nlu_result.get(
01773:                 "needsClarification"
01774:             )
01775:             or not nlu_result.get(
01776:                 "conversationComplete",
01777:                 False,
01778:             )
01779:         )
01780: 
01781:         if should_preserve:
01782:             await self._save_conversation_payload(
01783:                 context,
01784:                 updated_payload,
01785:             )
01786:         else:
01787:             await self._delete_conversation_state(
01788:                 context
01789:             )
01790: 
01791:         return response
01792: 
01793:     @staticmethod
01794:     def _merge_menu_days(
01795:         existing: Any,
01796:         new_days: list[Any],
01797:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
01798:         merged: dict[
01799:             str,
01800:             dict[str, Any],
01801:         ] = {}
01802: 
01803:         if isinstance(existing, list):
01804:             for day in existing:
01805:                 if not isinstance(day, dict):
01806:                     continue
01807: 
01808:                 key = str(
01809:                     day.get("date")
01810:                     or day.get("dayOfWeek")
01811:                     or len(merged)
01812:                 )
01813:                 merged[key] = dict(day)
01814: 
01815:         for day in new_days:
01816:             if not isinstance(day, dict):
01817:                 continue
01818: 
01819:             key = str(
01820:                 day.get("date")
01821:                 or day.get("dayOfWeek")
01822:                 or len(merged)
01823:             )
01824:             merged[key] = dict(day)
01825: 
01826:         return list(merged.values())[-14:]
01827: 
01828:     @staticmethod
01829:     def _infer_menu_date(
01830:         text: str,
01831:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01832:     ) -> str | None:
01833:         normalized = normalize_nlu_text(
01834:             text
01835:         )
01836: 
01837:         recent_menus = (
01838:             conversation_payload.get(
01839:                 "recentMenus"
01840:             )
01841:             or []
01842:         )
01843: 
01844:         for day in recent_menus:
01845:             if not isinstance(day, dict):
01846:                 continue
01847: 
01848:             date_value = str(
01849:                 day.get("date") or ""
01850:             ).strip()
01851:             day_name = normalize_nlu_text(
01852:                 day.get("dayOfWeek")
01853:                 or ""
01854:             )
01855: 
01856:             if (
01857:                 date_value
01858:                 and date_value in normalized
01859:             ):
01860:                 return date_value
01861: 
01862:             if (
01863:                 day_name
01864:                 and day_name in normalized
01865:             ):
01866:                 return date_value or None
01867: 
01868:         return None
01869: 
01870:     @staticmethod
01871:     def _rejected_proposal_reply(
01872:         proposal: dict[str, Any],
01873:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
01874:     ) -> str:
01875:         action_type = str(
01876:             proposal.get("actionType")
01877:             or ""
01878:         )
01879:         data = (
01880:             proposal.get("data")
01881:             if isinstance(
01882:                 proposal.get("data"),
01883:                 dict,
01884:             )
01885:             else {}
01886:         )
01887: 
01888:         if action_type == "RESERVE_FOOD":
01889:             food = (
01890:                 data.get("food")
01891:                 if isinstance(
01892:                     data.get("food"),
01893:                     dict,
01894:                 )
01895:                 else {}
01896:             )
01897:             food_name = str(
01898:                 food.get("foodName")
01899:                 or "این غذا"
01900:             )
01901:             target_date = str(
01902:                 data.get("date")
01903:                 or conversation_payload.get(
01904:                     "activeDate"
01905:                 )
01906:                 or "این روز"
01907:             )
01908:             target_label = str(
01909:                 data.get("dayOfWeek")
01910:                 or target_date
01911:             )
01912: 
01913:             alternatives = (
01914:                 conversation_payload.get(
01915:                     "recentMenus"
01916:                 )
01917:                 or []
01918:             )
01919:             other_dates: list[str] = []
01920: 
01921:             for day in alternatives:
01922:                 if not isinstance(day, dict):
01923:                     continue
01924: 
01925:                 day_date = str(
01926:                     day.get("date") or ""
01927:                 )
01928: 
01929:                 if (
01930:                     not day_date
01931:                     or day_date == target_date
01932:                 ):
01933:                     continue
01934: 
01935:                 foods = day.get("foods") or []
01936: 
01937:                 if any(
01938:                     normalize_nlu_text(
01939:                         item.get("foodName")
01940:                         or ""
01941:                     )
01942:                     == normalize_nlu_text(
01943:                         food_name
01944:                     )
01945:                     for item in foods
01946:                     if isinstance(item, dict)
01947:                 ):
01948:                     other_dates.append(
01949:                         str(
01950:                             day.get("dayOfWeek")
01951:                             or day_date
01952:                         )
01953:                     )
01954: 
01955:             other_date_text = (
01956:                 other_dates[0]
01957:                 if other_dates
01958:                 else "روز دیگری"
01959:             )
01960: 
01961:             return (
01962:                 f"باشه، «{food_name}» "
01963:                 f"{target_label} را رزرو نمی‌کنم. "
01964:                 f"منظورتان {other_date_text} بود "
01965:                 f"یا غذای دیگری برای {target_label}؟"
01966:             )
01967: 
01968:         return (
01969:             "باشه، پیشنهاد فعلی اجرا نمی‌شود. "
01970:             "جزئیات جایگزین را بفرمایید."
01971:         )
01972: 
01973:     @staticmethod
01974:     def _pending_action_reply_kind(
01975:         text: str,
01976:     ) -> str | None:
01977:         normalized = normalize_persian_text(
01978:             text
01979:         )
01980: 
01981:         normalized = re.sub(
01982:             r"[،,؛;.!؟?]+",
01983:             " ",
01984:             normalized,
01985:         )
01986: 
01987:         normalized = re.sub(
01988:             r"\s+",
01989:             " ",
01990:             normalized,
01991:         ).strip()
01992: 
01993:         confirm_phrases = {
01994:             "بله",
01995:             "اره",
01996:             "آره",
01997:             "اوکی",
01998:             "باشه",
01999:             "تایید",
02000:             "تأیید",
02001:             "تایید میکنم",
02002:             "تأیید میکنم",
02003:             "تایید می کنم",
02004:             "تأیید می کنم",
02005:             "انجام بده",
02006:             "ادامه بده",
02007:             "بله انجام بده",
02008:             "بله تایید میکنم",
02009:             "بله تأیید میکنم",
02010:             "بله لطفا",
02011:             "بله لطفاً",
02012:             "yes",
02013:             "ok",
02014:             "confirm",
02015:         }
02016: 
02017:         reject_phrases = {
02018:             "نه",
02019:             "خیر",
02020:             "نه ممنون",
02021:             "خیر ممنون",
02022:             "no",
02023:         }
02024: 
02025:         cancel_phrases = {
02026:             "لغو",
02027:             "لغو کن",
02028:             "حذف",
02029:             "حذف کن",
02030:             "کنسل",
02031:             "کنسل کن",
02032:             "بیخیال",
02033:             "بی خیال",
02034:             "انصراف",
02035:             "نمیخوام",
02036:             "نمی خواهم",
02037:             "نمیخوامش",
02038:             "cancel",
02039:         }
02040: 
02041:         if normalized in confirm_phrases:
02042:             return "confirm"
02043: 
02044:         if normalized in reject_phrases:
02045:             return "reject"
02046: 
02047:         if normalized in cancel_phrases:
02048:             return "cancel"
02049: 
02050:         return None
02051: 
02052:     @staticmethod
02053:     def _action_success_reply(
02054:         action_type: str,
02055:     ) -> str:
02056:         messages = {
02057:             "RESERVE_FOOD": (
02058:                 "رزرو غذا با موفقیت انجام شد."
02059:             ),
02060:             "CANCEL_FOOD": (
02061:                 "رزرو غذا با موفقیت لغو شد."
02062:             ),
02063:             "RATE_FOOD": (
02064:                 "امتیاز غذا با موفقیت ثبت شد."
02065:             ),
02066:             "CREATE_LEAVE": (
02067:                 "✅ درخواست مرخصی با موفقیت ثبت شد."
02068:             ),
02069:             "DELETE_LEAVE": (
02070:                 "درخواست با موفقیت حذف شد."
02071:             ),
02072:             "CREATE_TIME_EVENT": (
02073:                 "تردد با موفقیت ثبت شد."
02074:             ),
02075:         }
02076: 
02077:         return messages.get(
02078:             action_type,
02079:             "عملیات با موفقیت انجام شد.",
02080:         )

02082:     async def _handle_pending_chat_state(
02083:         self,
02084:         context: AgentContext,
02085:         text: str,
02086:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
02087:         employee_id = str(
02088:             context.employee_id or ""
02089:         ).strip()
02090: 
02091:         if not employee_id:
02092:             return None
02093: 
02094:         conversation_id = (
02095:             context.conversation_id
02096:         )
02097: 
02098:         state = await asyncio.to_thread(
02099:             self.repository.get_chat_state,
02100:             employee_id,
02101:             conversation_id,
02102:         )
02103: 
02104:         if not state:
02105:             return None
02106: 
02107:         state_type = str(
02108:             state.get("stateType") or ""
02109:         ).strip()
02110:         payload = state.get("payload") or {}
02111: 
02112:         if not isinstance(payload, dict):
02113:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02114:                 self.repository.delete_chat_state,
02115:                 employee_id,
02116:                 conversation_id,
02117:             )
02118:             return None
02119: 
02120:         if state_type == CONVERSATION_STATE:
02121:             return None
02122: 
02123:         if state_type not in {
02124:             FOOD_RESTAURANT_CONFIRM_STATE,
02125:             FOOD_RESTAURANT_SELECT_STATE,
02126:         }:
02127:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02128:                 self.repository.delete_chat_state,
02129:                 employee_id,
02130:                 conversation_id,
02131:             )
02132:             return None
02133: 
02134:         restaurants = payload.get(
02135:             "restaurants"
02136:         ) or []
02137:         restaurants = [
02138:             item
02139:             for item in restaurants
02140:             if isinstance(item, dict)
02141:             and parse_int(
02142:                 item.get("restaurantId")
02143:             )
02144:         ]
02145: 
02146:         if not restaurants:
02147:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02148:                 self.repository.delete_chat_state,
02149:                 employee_id,
02150:                 conversation_id,
02151:             )
02152:             return None
02153: 
02154:         reply_kind = (
02155:             self._pending_action_reply_kind(
02156:                 text
02157:             )
02158:         )
02159: 
02160:         if reply_kind in {
02161:             "reject",
02162:             "cancel",
02163:         }:
02164:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02165:                 self.repository.delete_chat_state,
02166:                 employee_id,
02167:                 conversation_id,
02168:             )
02169: 
02170:             previous_payload = payload.get(
02171:                 "conversationPayload"
02172:             )
02173: 
02174:             if isinstance(
02175:                 previous_payload,
02176:                 dict,
02177:             ) and previous_payload:
02178:                 await self._save_conversation_payload(
02179:                     context,
02180:                     previous_payload,
02181:                 )
02182: 
02183:             return {
02184:                 "reply": (
02185:                     "باشه، این رستوران انتخاب نشد. "
02186:                     "می‌توانید درخواست دیگری بفرستید."
02187:                     if reply_kind == "reject"
02188:                     else (
02189:                         "فرایند انتخاب رستوران "
02190:                         "لغو شد."
02191:                     )
02192:                 ),
02193:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
02194:             }
02195: 
02196:         selected_restaurant = (
02197:             self._match_restaurant_reply(
02198:                 text,
02199:                 restaurants,
02200:             )
02201:         )
02202: 
02203:         if (
02204:             state_type
02205:             == FOOD_RESTAURANT_CONFIRM_STATE
02206:             and selected_restaurant is None
02207:             and self._is_positive_reply(text)
02208:         ):
02209:             selected_restaurant = (
02210:                 restaurants[0]
02211:             )
02212: 
02213:         if selected_restaurant is not None:
02214:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02215:                 self.repository.delete_chat_state,
02216:                 employee_id,
02217:                 conversation_id,
02218:             )
02219: 
02220:             return await self._resume_food_request(
02221:                 context,
02222:                 payload,
02223:                 selected_restaurant,
02224:             )
02225: 
02226:         if self._looks_like_new_request(
02227:             text
02228:         ):
02229:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02230:                 self.repository.delete_chat_state,
02231:                 employee_id,
02232:                 conversation_id,
02233:             )
02234:             return None
02235: 
02236:         if (
02237:             state_type
02238:             == FOOD_RESTAURANT_CONFIRM_STATE
02239:         ):
02240:             restaurant = restaurants[0]
02241: 
02242:             return {
02243:                 "reply": (
02244:                     f"رستوران مجاز شما "
02245:                     f"«{restaurant.get('restaurantName')}» است. "
02246:                     "برای ادامه فقط «بله» یا «خیر» بفرستید."
02247:                 ),
02248:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
02249:                 "data": {
02250:                     "awaitingRestaurantConfirmation": True,
02251:                     "restaurant": restaurant,
02252:                 },
02253:             }
02254: 
02255:         return {
02256:             "reply": self._restaurant_selection_prompt(
02257:                 restaurants
02258:             ),
02259:             "requiresConfirmation": False,
02260:             "data": {
02261:                 "awaitingRestaurantSelection": True,
02262:                 "restaurants": restaurants,
02263:             },
02264:         }
02265: 
02266:     async def _resume_food_request(
02267:         self,
02268:         context: AgentContext,
02269:         payload: dict[str, Any],
02270:         restaurant: dict[str, Any],
02271:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
02272:         restaurant_id = parse_int(
02273:             restaurant.get("restaurantId")
02274:         )
02275: 
02276:         if restaurant_id is None:
02277:             raise AppError(
02278:                 "شناسه رستوران انتخاب‌شده معتبر نیست.",
02279:                 code="INVALID_RESTAURANT",
02280:             )
02281: 
02282:         original_message = str(
02283:             payload.get("originalMessage")
02284:             or "منوی غذا را نشان بده"
02285:         )
02286:         original_text = normalize_persian_text(
02287:             original_message
02288:         )
02289: 
02290:         target_date = payload.get(
02291:             "targetDate"
02292:         )
02293:         context_date = payload.get(
02294:             "contextDate"
02295:         )
02296: 
02297:         context_updates: dict[str, Any] = {
02298:             "restaurant_id": restaurant_id,
02299:             "meal_id": FOOD_MEAL_ID,
02300:         }
02301: 
02302:         if context_date:
02303:             context_updates["date"] = (
02304:                 context_date
02305:             )
02306: 
02307:         food_context = context.model_copy(
02308:             update=context_updates
02309:         )
02310: 
02311:         dates = (
02312:             [str(target_date)]
02313:             if target_date
02314:             else []
02315:         )
02316: 
02317:         conversation_payload = (
02318:             payload.get(
02319:                 "conversationPayload"
02320:             )
02321:             if isinstance(
02322:                 payload.get(
02323:                     "conversationPayload"
02324:                 ),
02325:                 dict,
02326:             )
02327:             else {}
02328:         )
02329: 
02330:         nlu_result = analyze_message(
02331:             original_message,
02332:             reference_date=str(
02333:                 food_context.date or ""
02334:             ),
02335:             conversation_state=(
02336:                 conversation_payload.get(
02337:                     "nluState"
02338:                 )
02339:                 if isinstance(
02340:                     conversation_payload.get(
02341:                         "nluState"
02342:                     ),
02343:                     dict,
02344:                 )
02345:                 else None
02346:             ),
02347:         )
02348: 
02349:         nlu_result = (
02350:             self._enrich_food_nlu_result(
02351:                 nlu_result,
02352:                 original_text,
02353:                 conversation_payload,
02354:             )
02355:         )
02356: 
02357:         response = await self._handle_food(
02358:             food_context,
02359:             original_text,
02360:             dates,
02361:             restaurant_validated=True,
02362:             selected_restaurant=restaurant,
02363:             conversation_payload=(
02364:                 conversation_payload
02365:             ),
02366:         )
02367: 
02368:         return await self._finalize_response_state(
02369:             food_context,
02370:             nlu_result,
02371:             conversation_payload,
02372:             response,
02373:             original_text,
02374:         )
02375: 
02376:     async def _begin_restaurant_selection(
02377:         self,
02378:         context: AgentContext,
02379:         text: str,
02380:         target_date: str | None,
02381:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
02382:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
02383:         employee_id = str(
02384:             context.employee_id or ""
02385:         ).strip()
02386: 
02387:         if not employee_id:
02388:             raise AppError(
02389:                 "کد پرسنلی کاربر برای دریافت رستوران‌ها مشخص نیست.",
02390:                 code="MISSING_EMPLOYEE_ID",
02391:             )
02392: 
02393:         conversation_id = (
02394:             context.conversation_id
02395:         )
02396: 
02397:         restaurants = (
02398:             await self.food.get_my_restaurants(
02399:                 context
02400:             )
02401:         )
02402: 
02403:         if not restaurants:
02404:             return {
02405:                 "reply": (
02406:                     "برای شما رستوران مجازی تعریف نشده است. "
02407:                     "لطفاً با واحد مربوطه تماس بگیرید."
02408:                 ),
02409:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
02410:                 "data": {
02411:                     "restaurants": [],
02412:                 },
02413:             }
02414: 
02415:         state_payload = {
02416:             "originalMessage": text,
02417:             "targetDate": target_date,
02418:             "contextDate": context.date,
02419:             "restaurants": restaurants,
02420:             "conversationPayload": (
02421:                 dict(
02422:                     conversation_payload
02423:                     or {}
02424:                 )
02425:             ),
02426:         }
02427: 
02428:         if len(restaurants) == 1:
02429:             restaurant = restaurants[0]
02430: 
02431:             await asyncio.to_thread(
02432:                 self.repository.save_chat_state,
02433:                 employee_id,
02434:                 FOOD_RESTAURANT_CONFIRM_STATE,
02435:                 state_payload,
02436:                 FOOD_CHAT_STATE_TTL_MINUTES,
02437:                 conversation_id,
02438:             )
02439: 
02440:             return {
02441:                 "reply": (
02442:                     f"رستوران مجاز شما "
02443:                     f"«{restaurant.get('restaurantName')}» است. "
02444:                     "منوی همین رستوران را بررسی کنم؟"
02445:                 ),
02446:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
02447:                 "data": {
02448:                     "awaitingRestaurantConfirmation": True,
02449:                     "restaurant": restaurant,
02450:                     "expiresInMinutes": (
02451:                         FOOD_CHAT_STATE_TTL_MINUTES
02452:                     ),
02453:                 },
02454:             }
02455: 
02456:         await asyncio.to_thread(
02457:             self.repository.save_chat_state,
02458:             employee_id,
02459:             FOOD_RESTAURANT_SELECT_STATE,
02460:             state_payload,
02461:             FOOD_CHAT_STATE_TTL_MINUTES,
02462:             conversation_id,
02463:         )
02464: 
02465:         return {
02466:             "reply": (
02467:                 self._restaurant_selection_prompt(
02468:                     restaurants
02469:                 )
02470:             ),
02471:             "requiresConfirmation": False,
02472:             "data": {
02473:                 "awaitingRestaurantSelection": True,
02474:                 "restaurants": restaurants,
02475:                 "expiresInMinutes": (
02476:                     FOOD_CHAT_STATE_TTL_MINUTES
02477:                 ),
02478:             },
02479:         }
02480: 
02481:     @staticmethod
02482:     def _restaurant_selection_prompt(
02483:         restaurants: list[dict[str, Any]],
02484:     ) -> str:
02485:         lines = [
02486:             "شما به چند رستوران دسترسی دارید. "
02487:             "لطفاً شماره یا نام رستوران موردنظر را بفرستید:"
02488:         ]
02489: 
02490:         for index, restaurant in enumerate(
02491:             restaurants,
02492:             start=1,
02493:         ):
02494:             restaurant_name = (
02495:                 restaurant.get("restaurantName")
02496:                 or "رستوران بدون نام"
02497:             )
02498:             lines.append(
02499:                 f"{index}. {restaurant_name}"
02500:             )
02501: 
02502:         return "\n".join(lines)
02503: 
02504:     @staticmethod
02505:     def _match_restaurant_reply(
02506:         text: str,
02507:         restaurants: list[dict[str, Any]],
02508:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
02509:         normalized_text = normalize_persian_text(
02510:             text
02511:         ).strip()
02512: 
02513:         compact_text = re.sub(
02514:             r"\s+",
02515:             " ",
02516:             normalized_text,
02517:         )
02518: 
02519:         for restaurant in restaurants:
02520:             restaurant_name = normalize_persian_text(
02521:                 str(
02522:                     restaurant.get("restaurantName")
02523:                     or ""
02524:                 )
02525:             ).strip()
02526: 
02527:             if not restaurant_name:
02528:                 continue
02529: 
02530:             short_name = restaurant_name.replace(
02531:                 "رستوران",
02532:                 "",
02533:             ).strip()
02534: 
02535:             if (
02536:                 restaurant_name == compact_text
02537:                 or restaurant_name in compact_text
02538:                 or (
02539:                     len(short_name) >= 3
02540:                     and short_name in compact_text
02541:                 )
02542:             ):
02543:                 return restaurant
02544: 
02545:         numbers = [
02546:             parse_int(value)
02547:             for value in re.findall(
02548:                 r"\d+",
02549:                 compact_text,
02550:             )
02551:         ]
02552:         numbers = [
02553:             value
02554:             for value in numbers
02555:             if value is not None
02556:         ]
02557: 
02558:         for number in numbers:
02559:             for restaurant in restaurants:
02560:                 if (
02561:                     parse_int(
02562:                         restaurant.get("restaurantId")
02563:                     )
02564:                     == number
02565:                 ):
02566:                     return restaurant
02567: 
02568:         for number in numbers:
02569:             if 1 <= number <= len(restaurants):
02570:                 return restaurants[number - 1]
02571: 
02572:         return None
02573: 
02574:     @staticmethod
02575:     def _is_positive_reply(text: str) -> bool:
02576:         normalized = normalize_persian_text(
02577:             text
02578:         ).strip()
02579: 
02580:         positive_phrases = {
02581:             "بله",
02582:             "اره",
02583:             "آره",
02584:             "اوکی",
02585:             "باشه",
02586:             "تایید",
02587:             "تأیید",
02588:             "ادامه بده",
02589:             "بررسی کن",
02590:             "انجام بده",
02591:         }
02592: 
02593:         return any(
02594:             phrase == normalized
02595:             or phrase in normalized
02596:             for phrase in positive_phrases
02597:         )
02598: 
02599:     @staticmethod
02600:     def _is_negative_reply(text: str) -> bool:
02601:         normalized = normalize_persian_text(
02602:             text
02603:         ).strip()
02604: 
02605:         negative_phrases = {
02606:             "خیر",
02607:             "نه",
02608:             "لغو",
02609:             "بیخیال",
02610:             "بی خیال",
02611:             "انصراف",
02612:             "نمیخوام",
02613:             "نمی خواهم",
02614:             "نمیخوامش",
02615:         }
02616: 
02617:         return any(
02618:             phrase == normalized
02619:             or phrase in normalized
02620:             for phrase in negative_phrases
02621:         )

02623:     def _looks_like_new_request(
02624:         self,
02625:         text: str,
02626:     ) -> bool:
02627:         return (
02628:             self._is_help(text)
02629:             or self._is_daily_brief(text)
02630:             or self._is_food(text)
02631:             or self._is_leave_balance(text)
02632:             or self._is_leave_list(text)
02633:             or self._is_delete_leave(text)
02634:             or self._is_leave_create(text)
02635:             or self._is_attendance(text)
02636:             or self._is_greeting(text)
02637:         )

02644:                 "راهنما",
02645:                 "کمک",
02646:                 "چه کار",
02647:                 "قابلیت",
02648:                 "چی بلدی",
02649:             ]
02650:         )

02658:             "صبح بخیر",
02659:             "عصر بخیر",
02660:             "شب بخیر",
02661:         }

02668:                 "گزارش امروز",
02669:                 "خلاصه امروز",
02670:                 "وضعیت امروز",
02671:                 "گزارش روزانه",

02675:     def _override_explicit_food_command(
02676:         self,
02677:         nlu_result: dict[str, Any],
02678:         text: str,
02679:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
02680:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
02681:         # FOOD_ROUTER_V4
02682:         result = dict(
02683:             nlu_result
02684:             or {}
02685:         )
02686: 
02687:         normalized = normalize_nlu_text(
02688:             text
02689:             or ""
02690:         )
02691: 
02692:         food_candidates = (
02693:             self._find_food_candidates(
02694:                 text,
02695:                 conversation_payload,
02696:             )
02697:         )
02698: 
02699:         has_candidate = bool(
02700:             food_candidates
02701:         )
02702: 
02703:         has_food_subject = any(
02704:             word in normalized
02705:             for word in [
02706:                 "غذا",
02707:                 "غذای",
02708:                 "غذاها",
02709:                 "منو",
02710:                 "ناهار",
02711:                 "خوراک",
02712:             ]
02713:         )
02714: 
02715:         reserve_phrases = [
02716:             "رزرو کن",
02717:             "رزرو شه",
02718:             "رزرو شود",
02719:             "ثبت کن",
02720:             "ثبت شه",
02721:             "ثبت شود",
02722:             "انتخاب کن",
02723:             "انتخاب شه",
02724:             "انتخاب شود",
02725:             "برام بگیر",
02726:             "برایم بگیر",
02727:             "سفارش بده",
02728:         ]
02729: 
02730:         show_phrases = [
02731:             "منو",
02732:             "نشان بده",
02733:             "نشون بده",
02734:             "نشون",
02735:             "نمایش بده",
02736:             "لیست کن",
02737:             "بگو",
02738:             "چیه",
02739:             "چی هست",
02740:             "چه غذایی",
02741:             "غذای امروز",
02742:             "غذای فردا",
02743:         ]
02744: 
02745:         cancel_phrases = [
02746:             "لغو کن",
02747:             "لغو شه",
02748:             "لغو شود",
02749:             "کنسل کن",
02750:             "حذف کن",
02751:         ]
02752: 
02753:         has_reserve_command = any(
02754:             phrase in normalized
02755:             for phrase in reserve_phrases
02756:         )
02757: 
02758:         has_show_command = any(
02759:             phrase in normalized
02760:             for phrase in show_phrases
02761:         )
02762: 
02763:         has_cancel_command = any(
02764:             phrase in normalized
02765:             for phrase in cancel_phrases
02766:         )
02767: 
02768:         requested_weekday = (
02769:             self._requested_weekday(
02770:                 text
02771:             )
02772:         )
02773: 
02774:         has_date_word = any(
02775:             word in normalized
02776:             for word in [
02777:                 "امروز",
02778:                 "فردا",
02779:                 "پس فردا",
02780:                 "پسفردا",
02781:                 "فروردین",
02782:                 "اردیبهشت",
02783:                 "خرداد",
02784:                 "تیر",
02785:                 "مرداد",
02786:                 "شهریور",
02787:                 "مهر",
02788:                 "آبان",
02789:                 "آذر",
02790:                 "دی",
02791:                 "بهمن",
02792:                 "اسفند",
02793:             ]
02794:         )
02795: 
02796:         last_domain_is_food = (
02797:             str(
02798:                 conversation_payload.get(
02799:                     "lastDomain"
02800:                 )
02801:                 or ""
02802:             )
02803:             == "FOOD"
02804:         )
02805: 
02806:         food_context = (
02807:             has_food_subject
02808:             or has_candidate
02809:             or last_domain_is_food
02810:         )
02811: 
02812:         intent = None
02813: 
02814:         if (
02815:             has_cancel_command
02816:             and food_context
02817:         ):
02818:             intent = "CANCEL_FOOD"
02819: 
02820:         elif (
02821:             has_reserve_command
02822:             and (
02823:                 food_context
02824:                 or bool(
02825:                     requested_weekday
02826:                 )
02827:                 or has_date_word
02828:             )
02829:         ):
02830:             intent = "RESERVE_FOOD"
02831: 
02832:         elif (
02833:             has_show_command
02834:             and (
02835:                 has_food_subject
02836:                 or bool(
02837:                     requested_weekday
02838:                 )
02839:                 or has_date_word
02840:             )
02841:         ):
02842:             intent = "SHOW_FOOD_MENU"
02843: 
02844:         if not intent:
02845:             return result
02846: 
02847:         entities = dict(
02848:             result.get("entities")
02849:             or {}
02850:         )
02851: 
02852:         inferred_date = (
02853:             self._infer_menu_date(
02854:                 text,
02855:                 conversation_payload,
02856:             )
02857:         )
02858: 
02859:         candidate_dates = {
02860:             str(
02861:                 item.get(
02862:                     "day",
02863:                     {},
02864:                 ).get("date")
02865:                 or ""
02866:             ).strip()
02867:             for item in food_candidates
02868:             if isinstance(
02869:                 item,
02870:                 dict,
02871:             )
02872:             and str(
02873:                 item.get(
02874:                     "day",
02875:                     {},
02876:                 ).get("date")
02877:                 or ""
02878:             ).strip()
02879:         }
02880: 
02881:         if (
02882:             not inferred_date
02883:             and len(candidate_dates) == 1
02884:         ):
02885:             inferred_date = next(
02886:                 iter(candidate_dates)
02887:             )
02888: 
02889:         if inferred_date:
02890:             entities["date"] = str(
02891:                 inferred_date
02892:             )
02893: 
02894:         state = dict(
02895:             result.get("state")
02896:             or {}
02897:         )
02898: 
02899:         state.update(
02900:             {
02901:                 "domain": "FOOD",
02902:                 "intent": intent,
02903:                 "entities": entities,
02904:                 "missingFields": [],
02905:             }
02906:         )
02907: 
02908:         return {
02909:             **result,
02910:             "domain": "FOOD",
02911:             "intent": intent,
02912:             "entities": entities,
02913:             "confidence": 0.99,
02914:             "missingFields": [],
02915:             "needsClarification": False,
02916:             "conversationComplete": False,
02917:             "clarification": None,
02918:             "errors": [],
02919:             "state": state,
02920:         }
02921:

02936:     def _is_leave_balance(text: str) -> bool:
02937:         return any(
02938:             keyword in text
02939:             for keyword in [
02940:                 "مانده مرخصی",
02941:                 "سهمیه مرخصی",
02942:                 "موجودی مرخصی",
02943:             ]
02944:         )
02945: 
02946:     @staticmethod
02947:     def _is_leave_list(text: str) -> bool:
02948:         read_words = [
02949:             "نمایش",
02950:             "نشان",
02951:             "لیست",
02952:             "ببین",
02953:             "وضعیت",
02954:             "درخواست های",
02955:             "درخواست‌های",
02956:         ]
02957:         leave_words = [
02958:             "مرخصی",
02959:             "ماموریت",
02960:             "مأموریت",
02961:         ]
02962:         mutation_words = [
02963:             "ثبت",
02964:             "حذف",
02965:             "لغو",
02966:             "کنسل",
02967:             "بزن",
02968:         ]
02969:         return (
02970:             any(
02971:                 word in text
02972:                 for word in read_words
02973:             )
02974:             and any(
02975:                 word in text
02976:                 for word in leave_words
02977:             )
02978:             and not any(
02979:                 word in text
02980:                 for word in mutation_words
02981:             )
02982:         )
02983:

02985:     def _is_delete_leave(text: str) -> bool:

02988:             for keyword in [
02989:                 "حذف",
02990:                 "کنسل",
02991:                 "لغو",
02992:             ]
02993:         ) and any(
02994:             keyword in text
02995:             for keyword in [
02996:                 "مرخصی",
02997:                 "ماموریت",
02998:                 "مأموریت",
02999:                 "درخواست",
03000:             ]
03001:         )

03036:     async def _handle_food(
03037:         self,
03038:         context: AgentContext,
03039:         text: str,
03040:         dates: list[str],
03041:         *,
03042:         restaurant_validated: bool = False,
03043:         selected_restaurant: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
03044:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
03045:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
03046:         conversation_payload = dict(
03047:             conversation_payload or {}
03048:         )
03049: 
03050:         requested_weekday = (
03051:             self._requested_weekday(text)
03052:         )
03053: 
03054:         target_date = (
03055:             dates[0]
03056:             if dates
03057:             else None
03058:         )
03059: 
03060:         # FOOD_WEEKLY_MENU_V2
03061:         normalized_food_text = (
03062:             normalize_nlu_text(text)
03063:         )
03064: 
03065:         menu_request_words = [
03066:             "منو",
03067:             "نشان بده",
03068:             "نشون بده",
03069:             "نشون",
03070:             "نمایش",
03071:             "لیست",
03072:             "ببین",
03073:             "بیار",
03074:             "چه غذایی",
03075:             "غذاها",
03076:         ]
03077: 
03078:         reservation_request_words = [
03079:             "رزرو",
03080:             "ثبت",
03081:             "انتخاب",
03082:             "برام بگیر",
03083:             "برایم بگیر",
03084:             "بگیرش",
03085:             "سفارش",
03086:         ]
03087: 
03088:         relative_date_words = [
03089:             "امروز",
03090:             "فردا",
03091:             "پس فردا",
03092:             "پسفردا",
03093:         ]
03094: 
03095:         has_explicit_numeric_date = bool(
03096:             re.search(
03097:                 r"(?:13|14)[0-9]{2}"
03098:                 r"\s*/\s*[0-9]{1,2}"
03099:                 r"\s*/\s*[0-9]{1,2}",
03100:                 normalized_food_text,
03101:             )
03102:         )
03103: 
03104:         has_explicit_named_date = bool(
03105:             re.search(
03106:                 r"[0-9۰-۹]{1,2}\s*"
03107:                 r"(?:فروردین|اردیبهشت|خرداد|"
03108:                 r"تیر|مرداد|شهریور|مهر|آبان|"
03109:                 r"آذر|دی|بهمن|اسفند)",
03110:                 normalized_food_text,
03111:             )
03112:         )
03113: 
03114:         has_relative_date = any(
03115:             word in normalized_food_text
03116:             for word in relative_date_words
03117:         )
03118: 
03119:         is_menu_request = any(
03120:             word in normalized_food_text
03121:             for word in menu_request_words
03122:         )
03123: 
03124:         is_reservation_request = any(
03125:             word in normalized_food_text
03126:             for word in reservation_request_words
03127:         )

03510:                     else None

03515:

03518:                 context,

03522:                     else None
