03635: 
03636:     @staticmethod
03637:     def _requested_weekday(
03638:         text: str,
03639:     ) -> str | None:
03640:         normalized = normalize_nlu_text(
03641:             text
03642:         )
03643: 
03644:         aliases = {
03645:             "شنبه": "شنبه",
03646:             "یکشنبه": "یکشنبه",
03647:             "یک شنبه": "یکشنبه",
03648:             "دوشنبه": "دوشنبه",
03649:             "دو شنبه": "دوشنبه",
03650:             "سه شنبه": "سه شنبه",
03651:             "سهشنبه": "سه شنبه",
03652:             "چهارشنبه": "چهارشنبه",
03653:             "چهار شنبه": "چهارشنبه",
03654:             "پنجشنبه": "پنجشنبه",
03655:             "پنج شنبه": "پنجشنبه",
03656:             "جمعه": "جمعه",
03657:         }
03658: 
03659:         matches: list[
03660:             tuple[int, int, str]
03661:         ] = []
03662: 
03663:         for alias, canonical in (
03664:             aliases.items()
03665:         ):
03666:             position = normalized.rfind(
03667:                 alias
03668:             )
03669: 
03670:             if position >= 0:
03671:                 matches.append(
03672:                     (
03673:                         position + len(alias),
03674:                         len(alias),
03675:                         canonical,
03676:                     )
03677:                 )
03678: 
03679:         if not matches:
03680:             return None
03681: 
03682:         return max(matches)[2]
03683: 
03684:     @classmethod
03685:     def _infer_menu_date_from_days(
03686:         cls,
03687:         text: str,
03688:         days: list[Any],
03689:     ) -> str | None:
03690:         requested_weekday = (
03691:             cls._requested_weekday(text)
03692:         )
03693: 
03694:         if not requested_weekday:
03695:             return None
03696: 
03697:         normalized_requested = (
03698:             normalize_nlu_text(
03699:                 requested_weekday
03700:             ).replace(" ", "")
03701:         )
03702: 
03703:         for day in days:
03704:             if not isinstance(day, dict):
03705:                 continue
03706: 
03707:             day_name = (
03708:                 normalize_nlu_text(
03709:                     day.get("dayOfWeek")
03710:                     or ""
03711:                 ).replace(" ", "")
03712:             )
03713: 
03714:             if (
03715:                 day_name
03716:                 and day_name
03717:                 == normalized_requested
03718:             ):
03719:                 date_value = str(
03720:                     day.get("date") or ""
03721:                 ).strip()
03722:                 return date_value or None
03723: 
03724:         return None
03725: 
03726:     @classmethod
03727:     def _filter_menu_days(
03728:         cls,
03729:         days: list[Any],
03730:         target_date: str | None,
03731:         text: str,
03732:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
03733:         safe_days = [
03734:             dict(day)
03735:             for day in days
03736:             if isinstance(day, dict)
03737:         ]
03738: 
03739:         if target_date:
03740:             return [
03741:                 day
03742:                 for day in safe_days
03743:                 if str(
03744:                     day.get("date") or ""
03745:                 )
03746:                 == str(target_date)
03747:             ]
03748: 
03749:         requested_weekday = (
03750:             cls._requested_weekday(text)
03751:         )
03752: 
03753:         if not requested_weekday:
03754:             return safe_days
03755: 
03756:         normalized_requested = (
03757:             normalize_nlu_text(
03758:                 requested_weekday
03759:             ).replace(" ", "")
03760:         )
03761: 
03762:         return [
03763:             day
03764:             for day in safe_days
03765:             if normalize_nlu_text(
03766:                 day.get("dayOfWeek")
03767:                 or ""
03768:             ).replace(" ", "")
03769:             == normalized_requested
03770:         ]
03771: 
03772:     @staticmethod
03773:     def _menu_reply(
03774:         days: list[dict[str, Any]],
03775:     ) -> str:
03776:         # FOOD_RECOMMENDATION_V3
03777:         if not days:
03778:             return (
03779:                 "برای تاریخ موردنظر "
03780:                 "منویی پیدا نشد."
03781:             )
03782: 
03783:         lines: list[str] = [
03784:             (
03785:                 "منوی این هفته:"
03786:                 if len(days) > 1
03787:                 else "منوی غذا:"
03788:             )
03789:         ]
03790: 
03791:         personal_best = None
03792:         public_best = None
03793: 
03794:         for day in days:
03795:             day_label = str(
03796:                 day.get("dayOfWeek")
03797:                 or day.get("date")
03798:                 or "این روز"
03799:             ).strip()
03800: 
03801:             date_value = str(
03802:                 day.get("date")
03803:                 or ""
03804:             ).strip()
03805: 
03806:             if (
03807:                 date_value
03808:                 and date_value
03809:                 not in day_label
03810:             ):
03811:                 label = (
03812:                     f"{day_label} "
03813:                     f"{date_value}"
03814:                 )
03815:             else:
03816:                 label = day_label
03817: 
03818:             foods = [
03819:                 dict(food)
03820:                 for food in (
03821:                     day.get("foods")
03822:                     or []
03823:                 )
03824:                 if isinstance(
03825:                     food,
03826:                     dict,
03827:                 )
03828:             ]
03829: 
03830:             selected_id = str(
03831:                 day.get(
03832:                     "selectedPlanDetailId"
03833:                 )
03834:                 or ""
03835:             ).strip()
03836: 
03837:             lines.append("")
03838:             lines.append(label)
03839: 
03840:             if selected_id:
03841:                 selected_name = next(
03842:                     (
03843:                         str(
03844:                             food.get(
03845:                                 "foodName"
03846:                             )
03847:                             or ""
03848:                         ).strip()
03849:                         for food in foods
03850:                         if str(
03851:                             food.get(
03852:                                 "planDetailId"
03853:                             )
03854:                             or ""
03855:                         ).strip()
03856:                         == selected_id
03857:                     ),
03858:                     "",
03859:                 )
03860: 
03861:                 lines.append(
03862:                     "✅ قبلاً رزرو "
03863:                     "کرده‌اید: "
03864:                     + (
03865:                         selected_name
03866:                         or "غذای رزروشده"
03867:                     )
03868:                 )
03869:                 continue
03870: 
03871:             available_count = 0
03872: 
03873:             for food in foods:
03874:                 food_name = str(
03875:                     food.get("foodName")
03876:                     or ""
03877:                 ).strip()
03878: 
03879:                 if not food_name:
03880:                     continue
03881: 
03882:                 remain_count = parse_int(
03883:                     food.get(
03884:                         "remainCount"
03885:                     )
03886:                 )
03887: 
03888:                 if (
03889:                     remain_count
03890:                     is not None
03891:                     and remain_count <= 0
03892:                 ):
03893:                     continue
03894: 
03895:                 available_count += 1
03896: 
03897:                 details = [
03898:                     f"• {food_name}"
03899:                 ]
03900: 
03901:                 interest = food.get(
03902:                     "personalInterestPercent"
03903:                 )
03904: 
03905:                 if interest is None:
03906:                     details.append(
03907:                         "علاقه شما: "
03908:                         "سابقه کافی نیست"
03909:                     )
03910:                 else:
03911:                     details.append(
03912:                         "علاقه شما: "
03913:                         f"{int(interest)}٪"
03914:                     )
03915: 
03916:                 public_percent = food.get(
03917:                     "publicReservationPercent"
03918:                 )
03919: 
03920:                 if (
03921:                     public_percent is not None
03922:                     and int(public_percent) > 0
03923:                 ):
03924:                     details.append(
03925:                         "رزرو عمومی: "
03926:                         f"{int(public_percent)}٪ "
03927:                         "از ظرفیت"
03928:                     )
03929: 
03930:                 average_rate = food.get(
03931:                     "averageRate"
03932:                 )
03933: 
03934:                 vote_count = (
03935:                     parse_int(
03936:                         food.get(
03937:                             "voteCount"
03938:                         )
03939:                     )
03940:                     or 0
03941:                 )
03942: 
03943:                 if (
03944:                     average_rate is not None
03945:                     and vote_count > 0
03946:                 ):
03947:                     try:
03948:                         rate_text = (
03949:                             f"{float(average_rate):g}"
03950:                         )
03951:                     except (
03952:                         TypeError,
03953:                         ValueError,
03954:                     ):
03955:                         rate_text = str(
03956:                             average_rate
03957:                         )
03958: 
03959:                     rating_text = (
03960:                         "امتیاز عمومی: "
03961:                         f"{rate_text} از ۵"
03962:                     )
03963: 
03964:                     if vote_count:
03965:                         rating_text += (
03966:                             f" ({vote_count} رأی)"
03967:                         )
03968: 
03969:                     details.append(
03970:                         rating_text
03971:                     )
03972: 
03973:                 if food.get(
03974:                     "isRecommended"
03975:                 ):
03976:                     details.append(
03977:                         "⭐ پیشنهاد برای شما"
03978:                     )
03979: 
03980:                     personal_best = {
03981:                         "name": food_name,
03982:                         "date": date_value,
03983:                         "day": day_label,
03984:                         "interest": interest,
03985:                     }
03986: 
03987:                 if (
03988:                     food.get(
03989:                         "isPublicFavorite"
03990:                     )
03991:                     and (
03992:                         (
03993:                             public_percent
03994:                             is not None
03995:                             and int(
03996:                                 public_percent
03997:                             ) > 0
03998:                         )
03999:                         or vote_count > 0
04000:                     )
04001:                 ):
04002:                     details.append(
04003:                         "🔥 محبوب‌ترین انتخاب عمومی"
04004:                     )
04005: 
04006:                     public_best = {
04007:                         "name": food_name,
04008:                         "date": date_value,
04009:                         "day": day_label,
04010:                         "publicPercent": (
04011:                             public_percent
04012:                         ),
04013:                         "averageRate": (
04014:                             average_rate
04015:                         ),
04016:                         "voteCount": (
04017:                             vote_count
04018:                         ),
04019:                     }
04020: 
04021:                 lines.append(
04022:                     " — ".join(
04023:                         details
04024:                     )
04025:                 )
04026: 
04027:             if not available_count:
04028:                 lines.append(
04029:                     "• غذای قابل رزروی "
04030:                     "اعلام نشده است."
04031:                 )
04032:

04034:             lines.extend(
04035:                 [
04036:                     "",

04038:                         "⭐ پیشنهاد شخصی هفته: "
04039:                         f"{personal_best['name']} "
04040:                         f"برای "
04041:                         f"{personal_best['day']} "
04042:                         f"{personal_best['date']}"
04043:                     ).strip(),
04044:                 ]
04045:             )
04046: 
04047:         if public_best:
04048:             public_parts = [
04049:                 (
04050:                     "🔥 محبوب‌ترین غذای "
04051:                     "قابل رزرو: "
04052:                     f"{public_best['name']}"
04053:                 )
04054:             ]
04055: 
04056:             if (
04057:                 public_best[
04058:                     "publicPercent"
04059:                 ]
04060:                 is not None
04061:                 and int(
04062:                     public_best[
04063:                         "publicPercent"
04064:                     ]
04065:                 ) > 0
04066:             ):
04067:                 public_parts.append(
04068:                     (
04069:                         f"{int(public_best['publicPercent'])}"
04070:                         "٪ ظرفیت آن رزرو شده"
04071:                     )
04072:                 )
04073: 
04074:             if (
04075:                 public_best[
04076:                     "averageRate"
04077:                 ]
04078:                 is not None
04079:                 and int(
04080:                     public_best.get(
04081:                         "voteCount"
04082:                     )
04083:                     or 0
04084:                 ) > 0
04085:             ):
04086:                 try:
04087:                     public_rate = (
04088:                         f"{float(public_best['averageRate']):g}"
04089:                     )
04090:                 except (
04091:                     TypeError,

04097:                         ]

04111:                         public_parts

04118:     @staticmethod
04119:     def _reservation_status_reply(
04120:         selected: list[dict[str, Any]],
04121:     ) -> str:
04122:         if not selected:
04123:             return (
04124:                 "رزرو فعالی برای تاریخ "
04125:                 "موردنظر پیدا نشد."
04126:             )
04127: 
04128:         lines = [
04129:             f"{len(selected)} رزرو فعال پیدا شد:"
04130:         ]
04131: 
04132:         for item in selected:
04133:             label = str(
04134:                 item.get("dayOfWeek")
04135:                 or item.get("date")
04136:                 or "این روز"
04137:             )
04138:             selected_id = str(
04139:                 item.get(
04140:                     "selectedPlanDetailId"
04141:                 )
04142:                 or ""
04143:             )
04144:             food_name = next(
04145:                 (
04146:                     str(
04147:                         food.get("foodName")
04148:                         or ""
04149:                     ).strip()
04150:                     for food in (
04151:                         item.get("foods") or []
04152:                     )
04153:                     if isinstance(food, dict)
04154:                     and str(
04155:                         food.get(
04156:                             "planDetailId"
04157:                         )
04158:                     )
04159:                     == selected_id
04160:                 ),
04161:                 "",
04162:             )
04163:             lines.append(
04164:                 f"- {label}: "
04165:                 f"{food_name or 'غذای رزروشده'}"
04166:             )
04167: 
04168:         return "\n".join(lines)
04169: 
04170:     @staticmethod
04171:     def _food_query_tokens(
04172:         text: str,
04173:     ) -> set[str]:
04174:         normalized = normalize_nlu_text(
04175:             text
04176:         )
04177:         tokens = set(
04178:             re.findall(
04179:                 r"[a-zA-Z]+|[0-9]+|"
04180:                 r"[\u0600-\u06FF]+",
04181:                 normalized,
04182:             )
04183:         )
04184: 
04185:         stop_words = {
04186:             "غذا",
04187:             "غذای",
04188:             "رزرو",
04189:             "کن",
04190:             "کنم",
04191:             "بده",
04192:             "بگیر",
04193:             "بگیرم",
04194:             "برام",
04195:             "برایم",
04196:             "برای",
04197:             "رو",
04198:             "را",
04199:             "لطفا",
04200:             "لطفاً",
04201:             "میخوام",
04202:             "میخواهم",
04203:             "سفارش",
04204:             "ثبت",
04205:             "امروز",
04206:             "فردا",
04207:             "پس",
04208:             "این",
04209:             "هفته",
04210:         }
04211: 
04212:         return {
04213:             token
04214:             for token in tokens
04215:             if token not in stop_words
04216:             and not token.isdigit()
04217:             and len(token) >= 2
04218:         }
04219: 
04220:     def _find_food_candidates(
04221:         self,
04222:         text: str,
04223:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
04224:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
04225:         query_tokens = self._food_query_tokens(
04226:             text
04227:         )
04228: 
04229:         if not query_tokens:
04230:             return []
04231: 
04232:         recent_menus = (
04233:             conversation_payload.get(
04234:                 "recentMenus"
04235:             )
04236:             or []
04237:         )
04238: 
04239:         candidates: list[
04240:             dict[str, Any]
04241:         ] = []
04242: 
04243:         for day in recent_menus:
04244:             if not isinstance(day, dict):
04245:                 continue
04246: 
04247:             for food in day.get(
04248:                 "foods"
04249:             ) or []:
04250:                 if not isinstance(food, dict):
04251:                     continue
04252: 
04253:                 food_name = str(
04254:                     food.get("foodName")
04255:                     or ""
04256:                 ).strip()
04257: 
04258:                 if not food_name:
04259:                     continue
04260: 
04261:                 normalized_name = (
04262:                     normalize_nlu_text(
04263:                         food_name
04264:                     )
04265:                 )
04266:                 name_tokens = set(
04267:                     re.findall(
04268:                         r"[a-zA-Z]+|[0-9]+|"
04269:                         r"[\u0600-\u06FF]+",
04270:                         normalized_name,
04271:                     )
04272:                 )
04273: 
04274:                 overlap = (
04275:                     query_tokens
04276:                     & name_tokens
04277:                 )
04278: 
04279:                 if (
04280:                     normalized_name
04281:                     in normalize_nlu_text(
04282:                         text
04283:                     )
04284:                     or overlap
04285:                 ):
04286:                     candidates.append(
04287:                         {
04288:                             "day": dict(day),
04289:                             "food": dict(food),
04290:                             "score": (
04291:                                 len(overlap)
04292:                                 + (
04293:                                     2
04294:                                     if normalized_name
04295:                                     in normalize_nlu_text(
04296:                                         text
04297:                                     )
04298:                                     else 0
04299:                                 )
04300:                             ),
04301:                         }
04302:                     )
04303: 
04304:         candidates.sort(
04305:             key=lambda item: (
04306:                 int(item.get("score") or 0),
04307:                 str(
04308:                     item.get("day", {}).get(
04309:                         "date"
04310:                     )
04311:                     or ""
04312:                 ),
04313:             ),
04314:             reverse=True,
04315:         )
04316: 
04317:         return candidates
04318: 
04319:     async def _prepare_named_food_reservation(
04320:         self,
04321:         context: AgentContext,
04322:         text: str,
04323:         candidates: list[dict[str, Any]],
04324:         target_date: str | None,
04325:         restaurant: dict[str, Any],
04326:         conversation_payload: dict[str, Any],
04327:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
04328:         inferred_date = (
04329:             self._infer_menu_date(
04330:                 text,
04331:                 conversation_payload,
04332:             )
04333:         )
04334:         preferred_date = (
04335:             target_date
04336:             or inferred_date
04337:             or str(
04338:                 conversation_payload.get(
04339:                     "activeDate"
04340:                 )
04341:                 or ""
04342:             ).strip()
04343:             or None
04344:         )
04345: 
04346:         preferred_candidates = [
04347:             item
04348:             for item in candidates
04349:             if (
04350:                 not preferred_date
04351:                 or str(
04352:                     item.get(
04353:                         "day",
04354:                         {},
04355:                     ).get("date")
04356:                     or ""
04357:                 )
04358:                 == preferred_date
04359:             )
04360:         ]
04361: 
04362:         if not preferred_candidates:
04363:             preferred_candidates = (
04364:                 candidates
04365:             )
04366: 
04367:         best_score = max(
04368:             int(
04369:                 item.get("score") or 0
04370:             )
04371:             for item in preferred_candidates
04372:         )
04373:         best_candidates = [
04374:             item
04375:             for item in preferred_candidates
04376:             if int(
04377:                 item.get("score") or 0
04378:             )
04379:             == best_score
04380:         ]
04381: 
04382:         distinct_ids = {
04383:             str(
04384:                 item.get("food", {}).get(
04385:                     "planDetailId"
04386:                 )
04387:                 or ""
04388:             )
04389:             for item in best_candidates
04390:         }
04391: 
04392:         if (
04393:             len(best_candidates) > 1
04394:             and len(distinct_ids) > 1
04395:             and all(
04396:                 str(
04397:                     item.get("day", {}).get(
04398:                         "date"
04399:                     )
04400:                     or ""
04401:                 )
04402:                 == str(
04403:                     best_candidates[0].get(
04404:                         "day",
04405:                         {},
04406:                     ).get("date")
04407:                     or ""
04408:                 )
04409:                 for item in best_candidates
04410:             )
04411:         ):
04412:             names = [
04413:                 str(
04414:                     item.get("food", {}).get(
04415:                         "foodName"
04416:                     )
04417:                     or "غذا"
04418:                 )
04419:                 for item in best_candidates
04420:             ]
04421: 
04422:             return {
04423:                 "reply": (
04424:                     "چند غذای مشابه پیدا شد: "
04425:                     + "، ".join(names)
04426:                     + ". نام دقیق غذا را بفرستید."
04427:                 ),
04428:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
04429:                 "data": {
04430:                     "candidates": (
04431:                         best_candidates
04432:                     ),
04433:                     "restaurant": restaurant,
04434:                 },
04435:             }
04436: 
04437:         selected = best_candidates[0]
04438:         day = selected["day"]
04439:         food = selected["food"]
04440: 
04441:         plan_detail_id = food.get(
04442:             "planDetailId"
04443:         )
04444: 
04445:         if not plan_detail_id:
04446:             raise AppError(
04447:                 "شناسه غذای انتخاب‌شده از API دریافت نشد.",
04448:                 code="MISSING_PLAN_DETAIL",
04449:             )
04450: 
04451:         selected_plan_detail_id = (
04452:             day.get(
04453:                 "selectedPlanDetailId"
04454:             )
04455:         )
04456: 
04457:         if selected_plan_detail_id:
04458:             if str(
04459:                 selected_plan_detail_id
04460:             ) == str(plan_detail_id):
04461:                 return {
04462:                     "reply": (
04463:                         "این غذا قبلاً برای "
04464:                         "این روز رزرو شده است."
04465:                     ),
04466:                     "requiresConfirmation": False,
04467:                     "data": {
04468:                         "date": day.get(
04469:                             "date"
04470:                         ),
04471:                         "food": food,
04472:                         "restaurant": restaurant,
04473:                     },
04474:                 }
04475: 
04476:             return {
04477:                 "reply": (
04478:                     "برای این روز قبلاً غذای "
04479:                     "دیگری رزرو شده است."
04480:                 ),
04481:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
04482:                 "data": {
04483:                     "date": day.get("date"),
04484:                     "food": food,
04485:                     "restaurant": restaurant,
04486:                 },
04487:             }
04488: 
04489:         remain_count = parse_int(
04490:             food.get("remainCount")
04491:         )
04492: 
04493:         if (
04494:             remain_count is not None
04495:             and remain_count <= 0
04496:         ):
04497:             return {
04498:                 "reply": (
04499:                     "ظرفیت این غذا تمام شده است."
04500:                 ),
04501:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
04502:                 "data": {
04503:                     "date": day.get("date"),
04504:                     "food": food,
04505:                     "restaurant": restaurant,
04506:                 },
04507:             }
04508: 
04509:         action = (
04510:             await self.actions.create_pending_action(
04511:                 str(context.employee_id),
04512:                 "RESERVE_FOOD",
04513:                 {
04514:                     "planDetailId": (
04515:                         plan_detail_id
04516:                     ),
04517:                     "restaurantId": (
04518:                         context.restaurant_id
04519:                     ),
04520:                     "mealId": (
04521:                         context.meal_id
04522:                         or FOOD_MEAL_ID
04523:                     ),
04524:                 },
04525:             )
04526:         )
04527: 
04528:         day_label = str(
04529:             day.get("dayOfWeek")
04530:             or day.get("date")
04531:             or "این روز"
04532:         )
04533:         food_name = str(
04534:             food.get("foodName")
04535:             or "این غذا"
04536:         )
04537: 
04538:         same_food_dates = {
04539:             str(
04540:                 item.get("day", {}).get(
04541:                     "date"
04542:                 )
04543:                 or ""
04544:             )
04545:             for item in candidates
04546:             if normalize_nlu_text(
04547:                 item.get("food", {}).get(
04548:                     "foodName"
04549:                 )
04550:                 or ""
04551:             )
04552:             == normalize_nlu_text(
04553:                 food_name
04554:             )
04555:         }
04556: 
04557:         if len(
04558:             {
04559:                 value
04560:                 for value in same_food_dates
04561:                 if value
04562:             }
04563:         ) > 1:
04564:             reply = (
04565:                 f"منظورتان «{food_name}» "
04566:                 f"{day_label} است؟ "
04567:                 f"برای {day_label} رزرو کنم؟"
04568:             )
04569:         else:
04570:             reply = (
04571:                 f"«{food_name}» را برای "
04572:                 f"{day_label} رزرو کنم؟"
04573:             )
04574: 
04575:         return {
04576:             "reply": reply,
04577:             "requiresConfirmation": True,
04578:             "pendingAction": {
04579:                 "id": action["id"],
04580:                 "label": (
04581:                     "تأیید و رزرو غذا"
04582:                 ),
04583:                 "expiresAt": action.get(
04584:                     "expiresAt"
04585:                 ),
04586:             },
04587:             "data": {
04588:                 "date": day.get("date"),
04589:                 "dayOfWeek": (
04590:                     day.get("dayOfWeek")
04591:                 ),
04592:                 "food": food,
04593:                 "restaurant": restaurant,
04594:                 "mealId": (
04595:                     context.meal_id
04596:                     or FOOD_MEAL_ID
04597:                 ),
04598:             },
04599:         }
04600: 
04601:     async def _prepare_cancel_food(
04602:         self,
04603:         context: AgentContext,
04604:         target_date: str | None,
04605:         restaurant: dict[str, Any],
04606:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
04607:         menu = await self.food.get_weekly_menu(
04608:             context
04609:         )
04610: 
04611:         for day in menu.get("days") or []:
04612:             if (
04613:                 target_date
04614:                 and day.get("date") != target_date
04615:             ):
04616:                 continue
04617: 
04618:             selected = day.get(
04619:                 "selectedPlanDetailId"
04620:             )
04621: 
04622:             if selected:
04623:                 action_payload = {
04624:                     "planDetailId": selected,
04625:                     "restaurantId": (
04626:                         context.restaurant_id
04627:                     ),
04628:                     "mealId": (
04629:                         context.meal_id
04630:                         or FOOD_MEAL_ID
04631:                     ),
04632:                 }
04633: 
04634:                 action = (
04635:                     await self.actions
04636:                     .create_pending_action(
04637:                         str(context.employee_id),
04638:                         "CANCEL_FOOD",
04639:                         action_payload,
04640:                     )
04641:                 )
04642: 
04643:                 return {
04644:                     "reply": (
04645:                         f"حذف رزرو غذا برای "
04646:                         f"{day.get('date')} آماده شد. "
04647:                         "پس از تأیید، حذف واقعی "
04648:                         "انجام می‌شود."
04649:                     ),
04650:                     "requiresConfirmation": True,
04651:                     "pendingAction": {
04652:                         "id": action["id"],
04653:                         "label": (
04654:                             "تأیید و حذف رزرو غذا"
04655:                         ),
04656:                         "expiresAt": action.get(
04657:                             "expiresAt"
04658:                         ),
04659:                     },
04660:                     "data": {
04661:                         "date": day.get("date"),
04662:                         "selectedPlanDetailId": (
04663:                             selected
04664:                         ),
04665:                         "restaurant": restaurant,
04666:                     },
04667:                 }
04668: 
04669:         return {
04670:             "reply": (
04671:                 "برای تاریخ گفته‌شده رزرو غذایی "
04672:                 "پیدا نشد."
04673:             ),
04674:             "requiresConfirmation": False,
04675:             "data": {
04676:                 "date": target_date,
04677:                 "restaurant": restaurant,
04678:             },
04679:         }
04680: 
04681:     async def _prepare_food_rating(
04682:         self,
04683:         context: AgentContext,
04684:         text: str,
04685:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
04686:         rate_match = re.search(
04687:             r"امتیاز\s*[:：]?\s*([1-5])"
04688:             r"(?:\s*از\s*5)?",
04689:             text,
04690:         )
04691: 
04692:         if rate_match is None:
04693:             rate_match = re.search(
04694:                 r"([1-5])\s*ستاره",
04695:                 text,
04696:             )
04697: 
04698:         rate = (
04699:             parse_int(rate_match.group(1))
04700:             if rate_match
04701:             else None
04702:         )
04703: 
04704:         if rate is None:
04705:             return {
04706:                 "reply": (
04707:                     "امتیاز را بین ۱ تا ۵ وارد کنید؛ "
04708:                     "مثلاً «به غذای آخر ۵ ستاره بده»."
04709:                 ),
04710:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
04711:             }
04712: 
04713:         reserve_match = re.search(
04714:             r"(?:رزرو|شناسه)\s*(\d+)",
04715:             text,
04716:         )
04717:         reserve_id = (
04718:             parse_int(reserve_match.group(1))
04719:             if reserve_match
04720:             else None
04721:         )
04722: 
04723:         history = await self.food.get_history(
04724:             context
04725:         )
04726: 
04727:         if reserve_id is None:
04728:             reserve_id = next(
04729:                 (
04730:                     parse_int(
04731:                         item.get("reserveId")
04732:                     )
04733:                     for item in history
04734:                     if parse_int(
04735:                         item.get("reserveId")
04736:                     )
04737:                 ),
04738:                 None,
04739:             )
04740: 
04741:         if reserve_id is None:
04742:             return {
04743:                 "reply": (
04744:                     "رزرو قابل امتیازدهی پیدا نشد."
04745:                 ),
04746:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
04747:             }
04748: 
04749:         comment_match = re.search(
04750:             r"(?:نظر|کامنت)\s*[:：]?\s*(.+)$",
04751:             text,
04752:         )
04753:         comment = (
04754:             comment_match.group(1).strip()
04755:             if comment_match
04756:             else ""
04757:         )
04758: 
04759:         action = (
04760:             await self.actions.create_pending_action(
04761:                 str(context.employee_id),
04762:                 "RATE_FOOD",
04763:                 {
04764:                     "reserveId": reserve_id,
04765:                     "rate": rate,
04766:                     "comment": comment,
04767:                     "isAnonymous": (
04768:                         "ناشناس" in text
04769:                     ),
04770:                 },
04771:             )
04772:         )
04773: 
04774:         return {
04775:             "reply": (
04776:                 f"ثبت امتیاز {rate} از ۵ برای "
04777:                 f"رزرو {reserve_id} آماده شد. "
04778:                 "تأیید می‌کنید؟"
04779:             ),
04780:             "requiresConfirmation": True,
04781:             "pendingAction": {
04782:                 "id": action["id"],
04783:                 "label": "تأیید و ثبت امتیاز",
04784:                 "expiresAt": action.get(
04785:                     "expiresAt"
04786:                 ),
04787:             },
04788:         }

04948:     async def _prepare_create_leave(
04949:         self,
04950:         context: AgentContext,
04951:         text: str,
04952:         dates: list[str],
04953:         times: list[str],
04954:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
04955:         target_dates = (
04956:             dates
04957:             or (

04998:             leave_type.get(
04999:                 "isAllowedDurationPartialDay"
05000:             )

05085:             if existing_rows
05086:             else ""
05087:         )

05109:                         existing_rows,
05110:                         list,

05112:                     else 0
05113:                 ),

05115:         }

05117:     async def _handle_attendance(
05118:         self,
05119:         context: AgentContext,
05120:         text: str,
05121:         dates: list[str],
05122:         times: list[str],
05123:         *,
05124:         force_create: bool = False,
05125:         event_type: str | None = None,
05126:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
05127:         target_dates = (
05128:             dates
05129:             or (
05130:                 [str(context.date)]
05131:                 if context.date
05132:                 else []
05133:             )
05134:         )
05135: 
05136:         is_create = (
05137:             force_create
05138:             or "ثبت" in text
05139:             or "بزن" in text
05140:         )
05141: 
05142:         if not is_create:
05143:             if not target_dates:
05144:                 return {
05145:                     "reply": (
05146:                         "برای بررسی تردد، تاریخ "
05147:                         "را وارد کنید."
05148:                     ),
05149:                     "requiresConfirmation": False,
05150:                 }
05151: 
05152:             end_date = (
05153:                 target_dates[1]
05154:                 if len(target_dates) > 1
05155:                 else target_dates[0]
05156:             )
05157: 
05158:             events = await self.hr.get_time_events(
05159:                 context,
05160:                 target_dates[0],
05161:                 end_date,
05162:             )
05163:             rows = events.get("data") or []
05164: 
05165:             return {
05166:                 "reply": (
05167:                     f"{len(rows) if isinstance(rows, list) else 0} "
05168:                     "تردد دریافت شد."
05169:                 ),
05170:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
05171:                 "data": {
05172:                     "events": rows,
05173:                 },
05174:             }
05175: 
05176:         if not target_dates or not times:
05177:             return {
05178:                 "reply": (
05179:                     "برای ثبت تردد، تاریخ و "
05180:                     "ساعت لازم است."
05181:                 ),
05182:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
05183:             }
05184: 
05185:         events = await self.hr.get_time_events(
05186:             context,
05187:             target_dates[0],
05188:             target_dates[0],
05189:         )
05190:         rows = events.get("data") or []
05191: 
05192:         valid, existing = (
05193:             self.hr.validate_time_event_distance(
05194:                 (
05195:                     rows
05196:                     if isinstance(rows, list)
05197:                     else []
05198:                 ),
05199:                 times[0],
05200:             )
05201:         )
05202: 
05203:         if not valid:
05204:             return {
05205:                 "reply": (
05206:                     f"تردد {existing} برای همین روز "
05207:                     "وجود دارد و فاصله کمتر از "
05208:                     "یک دقیقه مجاز نیست."
05209:                 ),
05210:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
05211:             }
05212: 
05213:         payload = {
05214:             "eventDate": target_dates[0],
05215:             "eventTime": times[0],
05216:             "eventType": event_type,
05217:             "note": "",
05218:         }
05219: 
05220:         action = (
05221:             await self.actions.create_pending_action(
05222:                 str(context.employee_id),
05223:                 "CREATE_TIME_EVENT",
05224:                 payload,
05225:             )
05226:         )
05227: 
05228:         event_type_label = {
05229:             "ENTRY": "ورود",
05230:             "EXIT": "خروج",
05231:         }.get(
05232:             str(event_type or ""),
05233:             "تردد",
05234:         )
05235: 
05236:         return {
05237:             "reply": (
05238:                 f"ثبت {event_type_label} برای "
05239:                 f"{target_dates[0]} ساعت "
05240:                 f"{self._human_time(times[0])} "
05241:                 "انجام شود؟"
05242:             ),
05243:             "requiresConfirmation": True,
05244:             "pendingAction": {
05245:                 "id": action["id"],
05246:                 "label": "تأیید و ثبت تردد",
05247:                 "expiresAt": action.get(
05248:                     "expiresAt"
05249:                 ),
05250:             },
05251:             "data": {
05252:                 "date": target_dates[0],
05253:                 "time": times[0],
05254:                 "eventType": event_type,
05255:             },
05256:         }
05257: 
05258:     @staticmethod
05259:     def _human_time(
05260:         value: str,
05261:     ) -> str:
05262:         match = re.fullmatch(
05263:             r"(\d{2}):(\d{2})",
05264:             str(value or "").strip(),
05265:         )
05266: 
05267:         if not match:
05268:             return str(value)
05269: 
05270:         hour = int(match.group(1))
05271:         minute = int(match.group(2))
05272: 
05273:         if hour == 0:
05274:             display_hour = 12
05275:             period = "بامداد"
05276:         elif hour < 12:
05277:             display_hour = hour
05278:             period = "صبح"
05279:         elif hour == 12:
05280:             display_hour = 12
05281:             period = "ظهر"
05282:         else:
05283:             display_hour = hour - 12
05284:             period = "شب"
05285: 
05286:         if minute:
05287:             return (
05288:                 f"{display_hour}:{minute:02d} "
05289:                 f"{period}"
05290:             )
05291: 
05292:         return f"{display_hour} {period}"
05293: 
05294:     @staticmethod
05295:     def _extract_additional_values(
05296:         text: str,
05297:         leave_type: dict[str, Any],
05298:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
05299:         values: dict[str, Any] = {}
05300:         definitions = (
05301:             leave_type.get(
05302:                 "toAdditionalFieldsDefinition"
05303:             )
05304:             or {}
05305:         ).get("results") or []
05306: 
05307:         for item in (
05308:             definitions
05309:             if isinstance(definitions, list)
05310:             else []
05311:         ):
05312:             if not isinstance(item, dict):
05313:                 continue
05314: 
05315:             key = (
05316:                 item.get("fieldName")
05317:                 or item.get("fieldname")
05318:                 or item.get("name")
05319:                 or item.get("field")
05320:             )
05321:             label = (
05322:                 item.get("fieldLabel")
05323:                 or item.get("label")
05324:                 or item.get("title")
05325:                 or ""
05326:             )
05327: 
05328:             if key and (
05329:                 "محل" in str(label)
05330:                 or str(key).upper()
05331:                 == "CUSTOMER02"
05332:             ):
05333:                 match = re.search(
05334:                     r"(?:محل|مکان)\s+([^،\n]+)",
05335:                     text,
05336:                 )
05337:                 values[str(key)] = (
05338:                     match.group(1).strip()
05339:                     if match
05340:                     else ""
05341:                 )
05342:             elif key:
05343:                 values[str(key)] = ""
05344: 
05345:         return values
05346: 
05347:     @classmethod
05348:     def _help_response(

05351:         return {

05360:             "suggestions": cls._suggestions(),
05361:         }

00025: def _is_sensitive_inspection_key(key: object) -> bool:
00026:     normalized = "".join(
00027:         character
00028:         for character in str(key).lower()
00029:         if character.isalnum()
00030:     )
00031: 
00032:     sensitive_parts = (
00033:         "authorization",
00034:         "token",
00035:         "password",
00036:         "usernamehash",
00037:         "passwordhash",
00038:         "digitcode",
00039:         "session",
00040:         "secret",
00041:         "cookie",
00042:         "apikey",
00043:     )
00044: 
00045:     return any(
00046:         part in normalized
00047:         for part in sensitive_parts
00048:     )
00049: 
00050: 
00051: def _sanitize_inspection_value(value: Any) -> Any:
00052:     if isinstance(value, dict):
00053:         sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
00054: 
00055:         for key, item in value.items():
00056:             if _is_sensitive_inspection_key(key):
00057:                 continue
00058: 
00059:             sanitized[str(key)] = (
00060:                 _sanitize_inspection_value(item)
00061:             )
00062: 
00063:         return sanitized
00064: 
00065:     if isinstance(value, list):
00066:         return [
00067:             _sanitize_inspection_value(item)
00068:             for item in value
00069:         ]
00070: 
00071:     if isinstance(value, tuple):
00072:         return [
00073:             _sanitize_inspection_value(item)
00074:             for item in value
00075:         ]
00076: 
00077:     return value
00078: 
00079: 
00080: def _mask_employee_id(
00081:     employee_id: str | None,
00082: ) -> str:
00083:     value = str(employee_id or "").strip()
00084: 
00085:     if not value:
00086:         return ""
00087: 
00088:     if len(value) <= 4:
00089:         return "*" * len(value)
00090: 
00091:     return (
00092:         "*" * (len(value) - 4)
00093:         + value[-4:]
00094:     )
00095: 
00096: 
00097: def _unwrap(payload: Any) -> Any:
00098:     current = payload
00099:     for _ in range(3):
00100:         if isinstance(current, dict) and "data" in current and len(current) <= 6:
00101:             current = current.get("data")
00102:             continue
00103:         break
00104:     return current
00105: 
00106: 
00107: def _as_list(payload: Any, *keys: str) -> list[Any]:
00108:     value = _unwrap(payload)
00109:     if isinstance(value, list):
00110:         return value
00111:     if isinstance(value, dict):
00112:         for key in keys:
00113:             candidate = value.get(key)
00114:             if isinstance(candidate, list):
00115:                 return candidate
00116:     return []
00117: 
00118: 
00119: def _detail_id(detail: dict[str, Any]) -> int | str | None:
00120:     return detail.get("id") or detail.get("planDetailId") or detail.get("planDetailID")

00128:     async def get_my_restaurants(
00129:         self,
00130:         context: AgentContext,
00131:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
00132:         """
00133:         دریافت و یکسان‌سازی فهرست رستوران‌های مجاز کاربر جاری.
00134: 
00135:         پاسخ سرویس Food معمولاً به شکل زیر است:
00136:         {
00137:             "data": [
00138:                 {
00139:                     "text": "رستوران یوتیلیتی",
00140:                     "value": "84"
00141:                 }
00142:             ]
00143:         }
00144:         """
00145: 
00146:         payload = await self.client.get_my_restaurants(
00147:             context
00148:         )
00149: 
00150:         rows = _as_list(
00151:             payload,
00152:             "items",
00153:             "results",
00154:             "restaurants",
00155:         )
00156: 
00157:         restaurants: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
00158:         seen_ids: set[int] = set()
00159: 
00160:         for item in rows:
00161:             if not isinstance(item, dict):
00162:                 continue
00163: 
00164:             restaurant_id = parse_int(
00165:                 item.get("value")
00166:                 or item.get("restaurantId")
00167:                 or item.get("resturantId")
00168:                 or item.get("id")
00169:             )
00170: 
00171:             if (
00172:                 restaurant_id is None
00173:                 or restaurant_id <= 0
00174:                 or restaurant_id in seen_ids
00175:             ):
00176:                 continue
00177: 
00178:             restaurant_name = str(
00179:                 item.get("text")
00180:                 or item.get("restaurantName")
00181:                 or item.get("resturantName")
00182:                 or item.get("name")
00183:                 or ""
00184:             ).strip()
00185: 
00186:             restaurants.append(
00187:                 {
00188:                     "restaurantId": restaurant_id,
00189:                     "restaurantName": (
00190:                         restaurant_name
00191:                         or f"رستوران {restaurant_id}"
00192:                     ),
00193:                 }
00194:             )
00195: 
00196:             seen_ids.add(restaurant_id)
00197: 
00198:         return restaurants

00200:     async def get_weekly_menu(self, context: AgentContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
00201:         payload = await self.client.get_food_plans_for_reserve(context)
00202:         days = _as_list(payload, "items", "days", "results")
00203:         return {
00204:             "restaurantId": context.restaurant_id or self.settings.default_restaurant_id,
00205:             "mealId": context.meal_id or self.settings.default_meal_id,
00206:             "days": [self._normalize_day(day) for day in days if isinstance(day, dict)],
00207:         }

00209:     async def get_history(self, context: AgentContext) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
00210:         payload = await self.client.get_my_food_reserve(context)
00211:         rows = _as_list(payload, "items", "results", "reserves")
00212:         return [self._normalize_history(item) for item in rows if isinstance(item, dict)]

00214:     async def reserve_food(self, context: AgentContext, plan_detail_id: int | str, *, idempotency_key: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
00215:         plan_id = parse_int(plan_detail_id)
00216:         if plan_id is None or plan_id <= 0:
00217:             raise AppError("شناسه غذای انتخاب‌شده معتبر نیست.", code="INVALID_PLAN_DETAIL")
00218:         menu = await self.get_weekly_menu(context)
00219:         target_day: dict[str, Any] | None = None
00220:         target_food: dict[str, Any] | None = None
00221:         for day in menu.get("days", []):
00222:             for food in day.get("foods", []):
00223:                 if str(food.get("planDetailId")) == str(plan_id):
00224:                     target_day, target_food = day, food
00225:                     break
00226:             if target_food:
00227:                 break
00228:         if target_food is None:
00229:             raise NotFoundError("غذای انتخاب‌شده در منوی فعلی پیدا نشد.")
00230:         selected = target_day.get("selectedPlanDetailId") if target_day else None
00231:         if selected:
00232:             if str(selected) == str(plan_id):
00233:                 raise ConflictError("این غذا قبلاً برای این روز رزرو شده است.", details={"planDetailId": plan_id})
00234:             raise ConflictError("برای این روز قبلاً غذای دیگری رزرو شده است.", details={"selectedPlanDetailId": selected})
00235:         remain = parse_int(target_food.get("remainCount"))
00236:         if remain is not None and remain <= 0:
00237:             raise ConflictError("ظرفیت این غذا تمام شده است.", details={"planDetailId": plan_id})
00238:         return await self.client.reserve_food(context, [plan_id], idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
00239: 
00240:     async def cancel_food(self, context: AgentContext, plan_detail_id: int | str, *, idempotency_key: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
00241:         plan_id = parse_int(plan_detail_id)
00242:         if plan_id is None or plan_id <= 0:
00243:             raise AppError("شناسه رزرو غذا معتبر نیست.", code="INVALID_PLAN_DETAIL")
00244:         menu = await self.get_weekly_menu(context)
00245:         selected_ids = {str(day.get("selectedPlanDetailId")) for day in menu.get("days", []) if day.get("selectedPlanDetailId")}
00246:         if str(plan_id) not in selected_ids:
00247:             raise ConflictError("این رزرو دیگر فعال نیست یا قبلاً حذف شده است.", details={"planDetailId": plan_id})
00248:         return await self.client.delete_food_reserve(context, plan_id, idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
00249: 
00250:     async def get_rating(self, context: AgentContext, *, reserve_id: int | str = 0, plan_detail_id: int | str = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
00251:         return await self.client.get_food_rate_comment(context, reserve_id=reserve_id, plan_detail_id=plan_detail_id)

00253:     async def set_rating(
00254:         self,
00255:         context: AgentContext,
00256:         *,
00257:         reserve_id: int | str,
00258:         rate: int,
00259:         comment: str,
00260:         is_anonymous: bool,
00261:         idempotency_key: str | None = None,
00262:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
00263:         safe_reserve_id = parse_int(reserve_id)
00264:         safe_rate = parse_int(rate)
00265:         if safe_reserve_id is None or safe_reserve_id <= 0:
00266:             raise AppError("شناسه رزرو برای ثبت امتیاز معتبر نیست.", code="INVALID_RESERVE_ID")
00267:         if safe_rate is None or safe_rate < 1 or safe_rate > 5:
00268:             raise AppError("امتیاز باید بین ۱ تا ۵ باشد.", code="INVALID_RATING")
00269:         return await self.client.set_food_rate_comment(
00270:             context,
00271:             reserve_id=safe_reserve_id,
00272:             rate=safe_rate,
00273:             comment=(comment or "").strip()[:1000],
00274:             is_anonymous=is_anonymous,
00275:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
00276:         )

00325:                     day.get("date")
00326:                     or ""

00331:             days = days[:max_days]
00332: 
00333:         def food_key(
00334:             value: Any,
00335:         ) -> str:
00336:             return " ".join(
00337:                 str(
00338:                     value
00339:                     or ""
00340:                 )

00344:                 )

00359:             if isinstance(
00360:                 item,

00362:             )
00363:             and food_key(
00364:                 item.get("foodName")
00365:             )
00366:         ]
00367: 
00368:         history_counter = Counter(
00369:             history_names
00370:         )
00371: 
00372:         max_history_count = (
00373:             max(
00374:                 history_counter.values()

00376:             if history_counter
00377:             else 0

00379:

00386:                 "selectedPlanDetailId"
00387:             ):

00391:                 day.get("foods")
00392:                 or []

00395:                     isinstance(

00399:                     and self._has_capacity(
00400:                         food
00401:                     )

00404:                         food

00418:                 context,

00420:                     :rating_limit

00424:         scored_items: list[

00801:             "publicRecommendation": (
00802:                 public_recommendation
00803:             ),
00804:         }
00805: 
00806: 
00807:     async def recommend_food(self, context: AgentContext, target_date: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
00808:         menu, history = await asyncio.gather(
00809:             self.get_weekly_menu(context),
00810:             self._safe_history(context),
00811:         )
00812: 
00813:         await self._write_weekly_inspection(
00814:             context=context,
00815:             menu=menu,
00816:             history=history,
00817:         )
00818: 
00819:         favorite_names = self._favorite_food_names(history)
00820:         target_days = menu["days"]
00821:         if target_date:
00822:             target_days = [day for day in target_days if day.get("date") == target_date]
00823:         already_reserved = [
00824:             {"date": day.get("date"), "selectedPlanDetailId": day.get("selectedPlanDetailId")}
00825:             for day in target_days
00826:             if day.get("selectedPlanDetailId")
00827:         ]
00828:         candidate_days = [day for day in target_days if not day.get("selectedPlanDetailId")]
00829:         candidates = [food for day in candidate_days for food in day.get("foods", []) if self._has_capacity(food)]
00830:         await self._enrich_ratings(context, candidates[: self.settings.max_rating_lookups])
00831: 
00832:         recommendations: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
00833:         for day in candidate_days:
00834:             available_foods = [food for food in day.get("foods", []) if self._has_capacity(food)]
00835:             best = self._best_food_candidate(available_foods, favorite_names)
00836:             if best:
00837:                 recommendations.append(
00838:                     {
00839:                         "date": day.get("date"),
00840:                         "dayOfWeek": day.get("dayOfWeek"),
00841:                         "food": best,
00842:                         "message": self._recommendation_message(day, best, favorite_names),
00843:                     }
00844:                 )
00845:         return {"recommendations": recommendations, "alreadyReserved": already_reserved, "menu": menu}
00846: 
00847:     async def _enrich_ratings(self, context: AgentContext, foods: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
00848:         semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(self.settings.rating_lookup_concurrency)
00849: 
00850:         async def enrich(item: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
00851:             plan_detail_id = item.get("planDetailId")
00852:             if not plan_detail_id:
00853:                 return
00854:             try:
00855:                 async with semaphore:
00856:                     rating = await self.get_rating(context, reserve_id=0, plan_detail_id=plan_detail_id)
00857:                 data = _unwrap(rating)
00858:                 item["ratingSummary"] = self._rating_summary(data if isinstance(data, dict) else {})
00859:             except Exception as exc:
00860:                 logger.warning("Food rating lookup failed plan_detail_id=%s error=%s", plan_detail_id, type(exc).__name__)
00861: 
00862:         await asyncio.gather(*(enrich(food) for food in foods))
00863: 
00864:     async def _safe_history(self, context: AgentContext) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
00865:         try:
00866:             return await self.get_history(context)
00867:         except Exception as exc:
00868:             logger.warning("Food history lookup failed error=%s", type(exc).__name__)
00869:             return []

00921:     def _normalize_day(self, day: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
00922:         details = day.get("details") or day.get("foods") or day.get("items") or []
00923:         foods = []
00924:         for detail in details if isinstance(details, list) else []:
00925:             if not isinstance(detail, dict):
00926:                 continue
00927:             foods.append(
00928:                 {
00929:                     "planDetailId": _detail_id(detail),
00930:                     "planId": detail.get("planId") or day.get("id") or day.get("planId"),
00931:                     "foodName": detail.get("foodName") or detail.get("name") or detail.get("title") or "",
00932:                     "description": detail.get("description") or detail.get("foodDescription") or "",
00933:                     "sideDishesName": detail.get("sideDishesName") or "",
00934:                     "imageName": detail.get("imageName") or detail.get("foodImageName"),
00935:                     "count": parse_int(detail.get("count")),
00936:                     "remainCount": parse_int(detail.get("remainCount")),
00937:                     "rate": parse_float(detail.get("rate")),
00938:                 }
00939:             )
00940:         return {
00941:             "planId": day.get("id") or day.get("planId"),
00942:             "date": day.get("date") or day.get("jalaliDate") or "",

00944:             "dayOfWeek": day.get("dayOfWeek") or day.get("dayName") or "",
00945:             "selectedPlanDetailId": day.get("selectedPlanDetailId") or day.get("selectedPlanDetailID"),
00946:             "foods": foods,

00949:     @staticmethod
00950:     def _normalize_history(item: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
00951:         return {
00952:             "reserveId": item.get("reserveId") or item.get("id"),
00953:             "planDetailId": item.get("planDetailId"),

00955:             "date": item.get("date") or item.get("reserveDate"),

00958:     @staticmethod
00959:     def _favorite_food_names(history: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[str]:
00960:         counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
00961:         for item in history:
00962:             name = str(item.get("foodName") or "").strip()
00963:             if name:
00964:                 counter[name] += 1
00965:         return [name for name, _ in counter.most_common(5)]
00966: 
00967:     @staticmethod
00968:     def _rating_summary(data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
00969:         rating_list = data.get("ratingList") or []
00970:         total_count = 0
00971:         weighted = 0
00972:         for row in rating_list if isinstance(rating_list, list) else []:
00973:             if not isinstance(row, dict):
00974:                 continue
00975:             stars = parse_int(row.get("id"))
00976:             count = parse_int(row.get("title"), 0)
00977:             if stars is None or count is None:
00978:                 continue
00979:             total_count += count
00980:             weighted += stars * count
00981:         fallback_rate = parse_float(data.get("rate"))
00982:         average = round(weighted / total_count, 2) if total_count else fallback_rate
00983:         comments = data.get("commentsList") or []
00984:         return {"averageRate": average, "voteCount": total_count, "commentsCount": len(comments) if isinstance(comments, list) else 0}
00985: 
00986:     @staticmethod
00987:     def _has_capacity(food: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
00988:         remain = parse_int(food.get("remainCount"))
00989:         return remain is None or remain > 0
00990: 
00991:     def _best_food_candidate(self, foods: list[dict[str, Any]], favorite_names: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
00992:         if not foods:
00993:             return None
00994:         favorite_set = {name.strip() for name in favorite_names}
00995:         scored: list[tuple[float, dict[str, Any]]] = []
00996:         for item in foods:
00997:             rating = item.get("ratingSummary") or {}
00998:             average = parse_float(rating.get("averageRate") or item.get("rate"))
00999:             vote_count = parse_int(rating.get("voteCount"), 0) or 0
01000:             remain = parse_int(item.get("remainCount"))
01001:             favorite_bonus = 1.5 if item.get("foodName") in favorite_set else 0
01002:             capacity_bonus = 0.3 if remain is None or remain > self.settings.low_capacity_threshold else 0
01003:             score = average + favorite_bonus + min(vote_count, 20) * 0.02 + capacity_bonus
01004:             scored.append((score, item))
01005:         return max(scored, key=lambda pair: pair[0])[1]
01006: 
01007:     def _recommendation_message(self, day: dict[str, Any], food: dict[str, Any], favorite_names: list[str]) -> str:
01008:         name = food.get("foodName") or "این غذا"
01009:         target_date = day.get("date") or "این روز"
01010:         rating = food.get("ratingSummary") or {}
01011:         average = parse_float(rating.get("averageRate") or food.get("rate"))
01012:         vote_count = parse_int(rating.get("voteCount"), 0) or 0
01013:         remain = parse_int(food.get("remainCount"))
01014:         parts = [f"برای {target_date}، «{name}» {'که قبلاً انتخابش کرده‌ای ' if name in set(favorite_names) else ''}گزینه پیشنهادی است."]
01015:         if average:
01016:             parts.append(f"امتیاز آن {average:g} از ۵" + (f" بر اساس {vote_count} رأی" if vote_count else "") + " است.")
01017:         if remain is not None and remain <= self.settings.low_capacity_threshold:
01018:             parts.append(f"فقط {remain} ظرفیت باقی مانده است.")
01019:         parts.append("در صورت تأیید، رزرو واقعی انجام می‌شود.")
01020:         return " ".join(parts)

00049: def normalize_text(value: Any) -> str:
00050:     if value is None:
00051:         return ""
00052: 
00053:     text = str(value).translate(_CHAR_TRANSLATION)
00054:     text = text.strip().lower()
00055: 
00056:     replacements = {
00057:         "پسفردا": "پس فردا",
00058:         "پس فردا": "پس فردا",
00059:         "رزروغذا": "رزرو غذا",
00060:         "رزروکنم": "رزرو کنم",
00061:         "رزروکن": "رزرو کن",
00062:         "ثبتکن": "ثبت کن",
00063:         "لغوکن": "لغو کن",
00064:         "ميخوام": "میخوام",
00065:         "می خواهم": "میخوام",
00066:         "می‌خواهم": "میخوام",
00067:         "می خوام": "میخوام",
00068:         "می‌خوام": "میخوام",
00069:         "مأموريت": "ماموریت",
00070:         "مأموریت": "ماموریت",
00071:         "ماموريت": "ماموریت",
00072:         "استعلاجي": "استعلاجی",
00073:         "استحقاقي": "استحقاقی",
00074:     }
00075: 
00076:     for old, new in replacements.items():
00077:         text = text.replace(old, new)
00078: 
00079:     text = re.sub(
00080:         r"\bدهو\s+",
00081:         "ده و ",
00082:         text,
00083:     )
00084: 
00085:     text = re.sub(
00086:         r"\s+",
00087:         " ",
00088:         text,
00089:     ).strip()
00090: 
00091:     return text
00092:

00094: def _tokens(text: str) -> list[str]:
00095:     return re.findall(
00096:         r"[a-zA-Z]+|[0-9]+|[\u0600-\u06FF]+",
00097:         normalize_text(text),
00098:     )
00099: 
00100: 
00101: def _contains_word(
00102:     text: str,
00103:     word: str,
00104: ) -> bool:
00105:     return normalize_text(word) in _tokens(text)
00106: 
00107: 
00108: def _contains_any_word(
00109:     text: str,
00110:     words: set[str] | tuple[str, ...] | list[str],
00111: ) -> bool:
00112:     text_tokens = set(_tokens(text))
00113: 
00114:     return any(
00115:         normalize_text(word) in text_tokens
00116:         for word in words
00117:     )
00118: 
00119: 
00120: def _contains_phrase(
00121:     text: str,
00122:     phrase: str,
00123: ) -> bool:
00124:     source_tokens = _tokens(text)
00125:     phrase_tokens = _tokens(phrase)
00126: 
00127:     if not phrase_tokens:
00128:         return False
00129: 
00130:     length = len(phrase_tokens)
00131: 
00132:     for index in range(
00133:         0,
00134:         len(source_tokens) - length + 1,
00135:     ):
00136:         if (
00137:             source_tokens[index:index + length]
00138:             == phrase_tokens
00139:         ):
00140:             return True
00141: 
00142:     return False
00143: 
00144:

00146:     text: str,

00148: ) -> bool:
00149:     return any(
00150:         _contains_phrase(text, phrase)

00159: _JALALI_LEAP_REMAINDERS = {
00160:     1,
00161:     5,
00162:     9,
00163:     13,
00164:     17,
00165:     22,
00166:     26,
00167:     30,

00171: def _is_jalali_leap(year: int) -> bool:
00172:     return year % 33 in _JALALI_LEAP_REMAINDERS
00173: 
00174: 
00175: def _jalali_month_length(
00176:     year: int,
00177:     month: int,
00178: ) -> int:
00179:     if 1 <= month <= 6:
00180:         return 31
00181: 
00182:     if 7 <= month <= 11:
00183:         return 30
00184: 
00185:     if month == 12:
00186:         return 30 if _is_jalali_leap(year) else 29
00187: 
00188:     raise ValueError("Invalid Jalali month.")
00189: 
00190: 
00191: def _parse_jalali_date(
00192:     value: str,
00193: ) -> tuple[int, int, int] | None:
00194:     normalized = normalize_text(value)
00195: 
00196:     match = re.fullmatch(
00197:         r"\s*(1[34][0-9]{2})[/-]"
00198:         r"([0-9]{1,2})[/-]"
00199:         r"([0-9]{1,2})\s*",
00200:         normalized,
00201:     )
00202: 
00203:     if not match:
00204:         return None
00205: 
00206:     year = int(match.group(1))
00207:     month = int(match.group(2))
00208:     day = int(match.group(3))
00209: 
00210:     if not 1 <= month <= 12:
00211:         return None
00212: 
00213:     try:
00214:         max_day = _jalali_month_length(
00215:             year,
00216:             month,
00217:         )
00218:     except ValueError:
00219:         return None
00220: 
00221:     if not 1 <= day <= max_day:
00222:         return None
00223: 
00224:     return year, month, day
00225: 
00226: 
00227: def _format_jalali_date(
00228:     year: int,
00229:     month: int,
00230:     day: int,
00231: ) -> str:
00232:     return f"{year:04d}/{month:02d}/{day:02d}"
00233: 
00234: 
00235: def _add_jalali_days(
00236:     value: str,
00237:     days: int,
00238: ) -> str:
00239:     parsed = _parse_jalali_date(value)
00240: 
00241:     if parsed is None:
00242:         return value
00243: 
00244:     year, month, day = parsed
00245: 
00246:     if days > 0:
00247:         for _ in range(days):
00248:             day += 1
00249: 
00250:             max_day = _jalali_month_length(
00251:                 year,
00252:                 month,
00253:             )
00254: 
00255:             if day > max_day:
00256:                 day = 1
00257:                 month += 1
00258: 
00259:                 if month > 12:
00260:                     month = 1
00261:                     year += 1
00262: 
00263:     elif days < 0:
00264:         for _ in range(abs(days)):
00265:             day -= 1
00266: 
00267:             if day < 1:
00268:                 month -= 1
00269: 
00270:                 if month < 1:
00271:                     month = 12
00272:                     year -= 1
00273: 
00274:                 day = _jalali_month_length(
00275:                     year,
00276:                     month,
00277:                 )
00278: 
00279:     return _format_jalali_date(
00280:         year,
00281:         month,
00282:         day,
00283:     )
