00385: )
00386: 
00387: _DAY_VALUE_PATTERN = (
00388:     rf"(?:[0-9]{{1,2}}|{_DAY_WORD_PATTERN})"
00389: )
00390: 
00391: 
00392: def _parse_jalali_day(value: str) -> int | None:
00393:     normalized = normalize_text(value)
00394:     normalized = re.sub(
00395:         r"\s+",
00396:         " ",
00397:         normalized,
00398:     ).strip()
00399: 
00400:     normalized = re.sub(
00401:         r"^([0-9]{1,2})\s*(?:ام|مین)$",
00402:         r"\1",
00403:         normalized,
00404:     )
00405: 
00406:     if normalized.isdigit():
00407:         day = int(normalized)
00408:         return day if 1 <= day <= 31 else None
00409: 
00410:     return _PERSIAN_DAY_WORDS.get(normalized)
00411: 
00412: 
00413: def _resolve_named_date_year(
00414:     text: str,
00415:     reference_date: str,
00416: ) -> int | None:
00417:     normalized = normalize_text(text)
00418: 
00419:     explicit_year_match = re.search(
00420:         r"(?<![0-9])(1[34][0-9]{2})(?![0-9])",
00421:         normalized,
00422:     )
00423: 
00424:     if explicit_year_match:
00425:         return int(explicit_year_match.group(1))
00426: 
00427:     parsed_reference = _parse_jalali_date(
00428:         reference_date
00429:     )
00430: 
00431:     if parsed_reference is None:
00432:         return None
00433: 
00434:     reference_year = parsed_reference[0]
00435: 
00436:     if _contains_any_phrase(
00437:         normalized,
00438:         {
00439:             "سال بعد",
00440:             "سال آینده",
00441:             "سال اینده",
00442:         },
00443:     ):
00444:         return reference_year + 1
00445: 
00446:     if _contains_any_phrase(
00447:         normalized,
00448:         {
00449:             "سال قبل",
00450:             "سال گذشته",
00451:             "سال پیش",
00452:         },
00453:     ):
00454:         return reference_year - 1
00455: 
00456:     return reference_year
00457: 
00458: 
00459: def _extract_named_jalali_date(
00460:     text: str,
00461:     reference_date: str,
00462: ) -> str | None:
00463:     normalized = normalize_text(text)
00464: 
00465:     patterns = (
00466:         # Examples: 9 مرداد / نهم مرداد / روز 9 مرداد ماه
00467:         rf"(?:\bروز\s+)?"
00468:         rf"(?P<day>{_DAY_VALUE_PATTERN})"
00469:         rf"(?:\s*(?:ام|مین))?"
00470:         rf"\s+"
00471:         rf"(?P<month>{_MONTH_NAME_PATTERN})"
00472:         rf"(?:\s*ماه)?",
00473: 
00474:         # Examples: مرداد 9 / مرداد ماه نهم
00475:         rf"(?P<month>{_MONTH_NAME_PATTERN})"
00476:         rf"(?:\s*ماه)?"
00477:         rf"\s+"
00478:         rf"(?:روز\s+)?"
00479:         rf"(?P<day>{_DAY_VALUE_PATTERN})"
00480:         rf"(?:\s*(?:ام|مین))?",
00481:     )
00482: 
00483:     for pattern in patterns:
00484:         match = re.search(
00485:             pattern,
00486:             normalized,
00487:         )
00488: 
00489:         if not match:
00490:             continue
00491: 
00492:         day = _parse_jalali_day(
00493:             match.group("day")
00494:         )
00495: 
00496:         month_name = normalize_text(
00497:             match.group("month")
00498:         )
00499:         month = _JALALI_MONTH_NAMES.get(
00500:             month_name
00501:         )
00502:         year = _resolve_named_date_year(
00503:             normalized,
00504:             reference_date,
00505:         )
00506: 
00507:         if (
00508:             day is None
00509:             or month is None
00510:             or year is None
00511:         ):
00512:             continue
00513: 
00514:         candidate = _format_jalali_date(
00515:             year,
00516:             month,
00517:             day,
00518:         )
00519: 
00520:         parsed = _parse_jalali_date(candidate)
00521: 
00522:         if parsed is not None:
00523:             return _format_jalali_date(*parsed)
00524: 
00525:     return None
00526: 
00527: 
00528: def _extract_explicit_jalali_date(
00529:     text: str,
00530:     reference_date: str,
00531: ) -> str | None:
00532:     normalized = normalize_text(text)
00533: 
00534:     matches = re.findall(
00535:         r"(?<![0-9])"
00536:         r"(1[34][0-9]{2})[/-]"
00537:         r"([0-9]{1,2})[/-]"
00538:         r"([0-9]{1,2})"
00539:         r"(?![0-9])",
00540:         normalized,
00541:     )
00542: 
00543:     for year_text, month_text, day_text in matches:
00544:         candidate = (
00545:             f"{year_text}/"
00546:             f"{month_text}/"
00547:             f"{day_text}"
00548:         )
00549: 
00550:         parsed = _parse_jalali_date(candidate)
00551: 
00552:         if parsed is not None:
00553:             return _format_jalali_date(*parsed)
00554: 
00555:     return _extract_named_jalali_date(
00556:         normalized,
00557:         reference_date,
00558:     )
00559: 
00560: 
00561: def _extract_relative_date(
00562:     text: str,
00563:     reference_date: str,
00564: ) -> str | None:
00565:     normalized = normalize_text(text)
00566: 
00567:     if _contains_phrase(
00568:         normalized,
00569:         "پس فردا",
00570:     ):
00571:         return _add_jalali_days(
00572:             reference_date,
00573:             2,
00574:         )
00575: 
00576:     if _contains_word(
00577:         normalized,
00578:         "فردا",
00579:     ):
00580:         return _add_jalali_days(
00581:             reference_date,
00582:             1,
00583:         )
00584: 
00585:     if _contains_word(
00586:         normalized,
00587:         "امروز",
00588:     ):
00589:         return reference_date
00590: 
00591:     if _contains_word(
00592:         normalized,
00593:         "دیروز",
00594:     ):
00595:         return _add_jalali_days(
00596:             reference_date,
00597:             -1,
00598:         )
00599: 
00600:     return None
00601: 
00602: 
00603: def _extract_date(
00604:     text: str,
00605:     reference_date: str,
00606: ) -> str | None:
00607:     explicit_date = (
00608:         _extract_explicit_jalali_date(
00609:             text,
00610:             reference_date,
00611:         )
00612:     )
00613: 
00614:     if explicit_date:
00615:         return explicit_date
00616: 
00617:     return _extract_relative_date(
00618:         text,
00619:         reference_date,
00620:     )
00621: 
00622: 
00623: # ============================================================
00624: # Persian number parsing
00625: # ============================================================
00626: 
00627: _BASE_NUMBER_WORDS = {
00628:     "صفر": 0,
00629:     "یک": 1,
00630:     "دو": 2,
00631:     "سه": 3,
00632:     "چهار": 4,
00633:     "پنج": 5,
00634:     "شش": 6,
00635:     "هفت": 7,
00636:     "هشت": 8,
00637:     "نه": 9,
00638:     "ده": 10,
00639:     "یازده": 11,
00640:     "دوازده": 12,
00641:     "سیزده": 13,
00642:     "چهارده": 14,
00643:     "پانزده": 15,
00644:     "شانزده": 16,
00645:     "هفده": 17,
00646:     "هجده": 18,
00647:     "نوزده": 19,
00648:     "بیست": 20,
00649:     "سی": 30,
00650:     "چهل": 40,
00651:     "پنجاه": 50,
00652:     "شصت": 60,
00653:     "هفتاد": 70,
00654:     "هشتاد": 80,
00655:     "نود": 90,
00656: }
00657: 
00658: _UNITS = {
00659:     1: "یک",
00660:     2: "دو",
00661:     3: "سه",
00662:     4: "چهار",
00663:     5: "پنج",
00664:     6: "شش",
00665:     7: "هفت",
00666:     8: "هشت",
00667:     9: "نه",
00668: }
00669: 
00670: _TENS = {
00671:     20: "بیست",
00672:     30: "سی",
00673:     40: "چهل",
00674:     50: "پنجاه",
00675:     60: "شصت",
00676:     70: "هفتاد",
00677:     80: "هشتاد",
00678:     90: "نود",
00679: }
00680: 
00681: 
00682: def _build_number_phrases(
00683:     maximum: int,
00684: ) -> dict[str, int]:
00685:     result: dict[str, int] = {}
00686: 
00687:     for word, number in _BASE_NUMBER_WORDS.items():
00688:         if number <= maximum:
00689:             result[word] = number
00690: 
00691:     for tens_number, tens_word in _TENS.items():
00692:         if tens_number > maximum:
00693:             continue
00694: 
00695:         result[tens_word] = tens_number
00696: 
00697:         for unit_number, unit_word in _UNITS.items():
00698:             value = tens_number + unit_number
00699: 
00700:             if value <= maximum:
00701:                 result[
00702:                     f"{tens_word} و {unit_word}"
00703:                 ] = value
00704: 
00705:     return result
00706: 
00707: 
00708: _HOUR_PHRASES = _build_number_phrases(24)
00709: _MINUTE_PHRASES = _build_number_phrases(59)
00710: 
00711: _HOUR_PATTERN = "|".join(
00712:     re.escape(value)
00713:     for value in sorted(
00714:         _HOUR_PHRASES,
00715:         key=len,
00716:         reverse=True,
00717:     )
00718: )
00719: 
00720: _MINUTE_PATTERN = "|".join(
00721:     re.escape(value)
00722:     for value in sorted(
00723:         _MINUTE_PHRASES,
00724:         key=len,
00725:         reverse=True,
00726:     )
00727: )
00728: 
00729: _HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN = (
00730:     rf"(?:[0-9]{{1,2}}|{_HOUR_PATTERN})"
00731: )
00732: 
00733: _MINUTE_VALUE_PATTERN = (
00734:     rf"(?:[0-9]{{1,2}}|{_MINUTE_PATTERN})"
00735: )
00736: 
00737: 
00738: def _parse_number_value(
00739:     value: str,
00740:     *,
00741:     maximum: int,
00742: ) -> int | None:
00743:     normalized = normalize_text(value)
00744:     normalized = re.sub(
00745:         r"\s+",
00746:         " ",
00747:         normalized,
00748:     ).strip()
00749: 
00750:     if normalized.isdigit():
00751:         number = int(normalized)
00752: 
00753:         if 0 <= number <= maximum:
00754:             return number
00755: 
00756:         return None
00757: 
00758:     phrase_map = (
00759:         _HOUR_PHRASES
00760:         if maximum <= 24
00761:         else _MINUTE_PHRASES
00762:     )
00763: 
00764:     number = phrase_map.get(normalized)
00765: 
00766:     if number is None:
00767:         return None
00768: 
00769:     if not 0 <= number <= maximum:
00770:         return None
00771: 
00772:     return number
00773: 
00774: 
00775: # ============================================================
00776: # Time extraction
00777: # ============================================================
00778: 
00779: _MERIDIEM_WORDS = {
00780:     "صبح": "AM",
00781:     "بامداد": "AM",
00782:     "ظهر": "PM",
00783:     "بعدازظهر": "PM",
00784:     "عصر": "PM",
00785:     "شب": "PM",
00786: }
00787: 
00788: 
00789: def _extract_meridiem(
00790:     text: str,
00791: ) -> str | None:
00792:     normalized = normalize_text(text)
00793: 
00794:     for word, value in _MERIDIEM_WORDS.items():
00795:         if _contains_word(normalized, word):
00796:             return value
00797: 
00798:     return None
00799: 
00800: 
00801: def _apply_meridiem(
00802:     hour: int,
00803:     meridiem: str | None,
00804: ) -> int:
00805:     if meridiem == "AM":
00806:         if hour == 12:
00807:             return 0
00808: 
00809:         return hour
00810: 
00811:     if meridiem == "PM":
00812:         if 1 <= hour <= 11:
00813:             return hour + 12
00814: 
00815:         return hour
00816: 
00817:     return hour
00818: 
00819: 
00820: def _format_time(
00821:     hour: int,
00822:     minute: int,
00823: ) -> str:
00824:     return f"{hour:02d}:{minute:02d}"
00825: 
00826: 
00827: def _time_result(
00828:     *,
00829:     time_value: str | None = None,
00830:     found: bool = False,
00831:     ambiguous: bool = False,
00832:     error: str | None = None,
00833:     hour: int | None = None,
00834:     minute: int | None = None,
00835:     meridiem: str | None = None,
00836: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
00837:     return {
00838:         "time": time_value,
00839:         "found": found,
00840:         "ambiguous": ambiguous,
00841:         "error": error,
00842:         "hour": hour,
00843:         "minute": minute,
00844:         "meridiem": meridiem,
00845:     }
00846: 
00847: 
00848: def _parse_time_segment(
00849:     segment: str,
00850: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
00851:     text = normalize_text(segment)
00852:     meridiem = _extract_meridiem(text)
00853: 
00854:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00855:     # HH:MM
00856:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00857: 
00858:     colon_match = re.search(
00859:         r"(?<![0-9/])"
00860:         r"([0-9]{1,2}):([0-9]{1,2})"
00861:         r"(?![0-9])",
00862:         text,
00863:     )
00864: 
00865:     if colon_match:
00866:         hour = int(colon_match.group(1))
00867:         minute = int(colon_match.group(2))
00868: 
00869:         if not 0 <= hour <= 23:
00870:             return _time_result(
00871:                 found=True,
00872:                 error="ساعت واردشده معتبر نیست.",
00873:             )
00874: 
00875:         if not 0 <= minute <= 59:
00876:             return _time_result(
00877:                 found=True,
00878:                 error=(
00879:                     "دقیقه باید عددی بین "
00880:                     "۰ تا ۵۹ باشد."
00881:                 ),
00882:             )
00883: 
00884:         hour = _apply_meridiem(
00885:             hour,
00886:             meridiem,
00887:         )
00888: 
00889:         return _time_result(
00890:             time_value=_format_time(
00891:                 hour,
00892:                 minute,
00893:             ),
00894:             found=True,
00895:         )
00896: 
00897:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00898:     # Three quarters
00899:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00900: 
00901:     three_quarter_match = re.search(
00902:         rf"(?:ساعت\s+)?"
00903:         rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})"
00904:         rf"\s+و\s+سه\s+ربع",
00905:         text,
00906:     )
00907: 
00908:     if three_quarter_match:
00909:         hour = _parse_number_value(
00910:             three_quarter_match.group("hour"),
00911:             maximum=24,
00912:         )
00913: 
00914:         if hour is None or not 0 <= hour <= 23:
00915:             return _time_result(
00916:                 found=True,
00917:                 error="ساعت واردشده معتبر نیست.",
00918:             )
00919: 
00920:         hour = _apply_meridiem(
00921:             hour,
00922:             meridiem,
00923:         )
00924: 
00925:         return _time_result(
00926:             time_value=_format_time(
00927:                 hour,
00928:                 45,
00929:             ),
00930:             found=True,
00931:         )
00932: 
00933:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00934:     # Half
00935:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00936: 
00937:     half_match = re.search(
00938:         rf"(?:ساعت\s+)?"
00939:         rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})"
00940:         rf"\s+و\s+نیم",
00941:         text,
00942:     )
00943: 
00944:     if half_match:
00945:         hour = _parse_number_value(
00946:             half_match.group("hour"),
00947:             maximum=24,
00948:         )
00949: 
00950:         if hour is None or not 0 <= hour <= 23:
00951:             return _time_result(
00952:                 found=True,
00953:                 error="ساعت واردشده معتبر نیست.",
00954:             )
00955: 
00956:         hour = _apply_meridiem(
00957:             hour,
00958:             meridiem,
00959:         )
00960: 
00961:         return _time_result(
00962:             time_value=_format_time(
00963:                 hour,
00964:                 30,
00965:             ),
00966:             found=True,
00967:         )
00968: 
00969:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00970:     # Quarter
00971:     # --------------------------------------------------------
00972: 
00973:     quarter_match = re.search(
00974:         rf"(?:ساعت\s+)?"
00975:         rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})"
00976:         rf"\s+و\s+ربع",
00977:         text,
00978:     )
00979: 
00980:     if quarter_match:
00981:         hour = _parse_number_value(
00982:             quarter_match.group("hour"),
00983:             maximum=24,
00984:         )
00985: 
00986:         if hour is None or not 0 <= hour <= 23:
00987:             return _time_result(
00988:                 found=True,
00989:                 error="ساعت واردشده معتبر نیست.",
00990:             )
00991: 
00992:         hour = _apply_meridiem(
00993:             hour,
00994:             meridiem,
00995:         )
00996: 
00997:         return _time_result(
00998:             time_value=_format_time(
00999:                 hour,
01000:                 15,
01001:             ),
01002:             found=True,
01003:         )
01004: 
01005:     # --------------------------------------------------------
01006:     # Hour and minute: 10 و 50 / ده و پنجاه
01007:     # --------------------------------------------------------
01008: 
01009:     hour_minute_match = re.search(
01010:         rf"(?:ساعت\s+)?"
01011:         rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})"
01012:         rf"\s+و\s+"
01013:         rf"(?P<minute>{_MINUTE_VALUE_PATTERN})"
01014:         rf"(?:\s+دقیقه)?",
01015:         text,
01016:     )
01017: 
01018:     if hour_minute_match:
01019:         hour = _parse_number_value(
01020:             hour_minute_match.group("hour"),
01021:             maximum=24,
01022:         )
01023: 
01024:         minute_text = (
01025:             hour_minute_match.group("minute")
01026:         )
01027: 
01028:         minute = _parse_number_value(
01029:             minute_text,
01030:             maximum=99,
01031:         )
01032: 
01033:         if minute is None:
01034:             raw_minute = normalize_text(
01035:                 minute_text
01036:             )
01037: 
01038:             if raw_minute.isdigit():
01039:                 minute = int(raw_minute)
01040: 
01041:         if hour is None or not 0 <= hour <= 23:
01042:             return _time_result(
01043:                 found=True,
01044:                 error="ساعت واردشده معتبر نیست.",
01045:             )
01046: 
01047:         if minute is None or not 0 <= minute <= 59:
01048:             return _time_result(
01049:                 found=True,
01050:                 error=(
01051:                     "دقیقه باید عددی بین "
01052:                     "۰ تا ۵۹ باشد."
01053:                 ),
01054:             )
01055: 
01056:         hour = _apply_meridiem(
01057:             hour,
01058:             meridiem,
01059:         )
01060: 
01061:         return _time_result(
01062:             time_value=_format_time(
01063:                 hour,
01064:                 minute,
01065:             ),
01066:             found=True,
01067:         )
01068: 
01069:     # --------------------------------------------------------
01070:     # Invalid written minute such as 75
01071:     # --------------------------------------------------------
01072: 
01073:     invalid_minute_match = re.search(
01074:         rf"(?:ساعت\s+)?"
01075:         rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})"
01076:         rf"\s+و\s+"
01077:         rf"(?P<minute>[0-9]{{1,3}}|"
01078:         rf"هفتاد(?:\s+و\s+\S+)?|"
01079:         rf"هشتاد(?:\s+و\s+\S+)?|"
01080:         rf"نود(?:\s+و\s+\S+)?)"
01081:         rf"\s+دقیقه",
01082:         text,
01083:     )
01084: 
01085:     if invalid_minute_match:
01086:         return _time_result(
01087:             found=True,
01088:             error=(
01089:                 "دقیقه باید عددی بین "
01090:                 "۰ تا ۵۹ باشد."
01091:             ),
01092:         )
01093: 
01094:     # --------------------------------------------------------
01095:     # Plain hour
01096:     # --------------------------------------------------------
01097: 
01098:     if meridiem is not None:
01099:         meridiem_pattern = (
01100:             r"صبح|بامداد|ظهر|بعدازظهر|عصر|شب"
01101:         )
01102: 
01103:         plain_hour_match = re.search(
01104:             rf"(?:ساعت\s+)?"
01105:             rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})"
01106:             rf"\s+(?:{meridiem_pattern})",
01107:             text,
01108:         )
01109:     else:
01110:         plain_hour_match = re.search(
01111:             rf"(?:ساعت\s+)"
01112:             rf"(?P<hour>{_HOUR_VALUE_PATTERN})",
01113:             text,
01114:         )
01115: 
01116:     if plain_hour_match:
01117:         hour = _parse_number_value(
01118:             plain_hour_match.group("hour"),
01119:             maximum=24,
01120:         )
01121: 
01122:         if hour is None or not 0 <= hour <= 23:
01123:             return _time_result(
01124:                 found=True,
01125:                 error="ساعت واردشده معتبر نیست.",
01126:             )
01127: 
01128:         if (
01129:             meridiem is None
01130:             and 1 <= hour <= 12
01131:         ):
01132:             return _time_result(
01133:                 found=True,
01134:                 ambiguous=True,
01135:                 hour=hour,
01136:                 minute=0,
01137:             )
01138: 
01139:         hour = _apply_meridiem(
01140:             hour,
01141:             meridiem,
01142:         )
01143: 
01144:         return _time_result(
01145:             time_value=_format_time(
01146:                 hour,
01147:                 0,
01148:             ),
01149:             found=True,
01150:         )
01151: 
01152:     return _time_result()
01153: 
01154: 
01155: def _extract_time_entities(
01156:     text: str,
01157: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
01158:     normalized = normalize_text(text)
01159:     meridiem = _extract_meridiem(
01160:         normalized
01161:     )
01162: 
01163:     range_match = re.search(
01164:         r"\bاز\b(?P<start>.+?)"
01165:         r"\bتا\b(?P<end>.+)",
01166:         normalized,
01167:     )
01168: 
01169:     if range_match:
01170:         start_result = _parse_time_segment(
01171:             range_match.group("start")
01172:         )
01173: 
01174:         end_result = _parse_time_segment(
01175:             range_match.group("end")
01176:         )
01177: 
01178:         errors = [
01179:             result["error"]
01180:             for result in (
01181:                 start_result,
01182:                 end_result,
01183:             )
01184:             if result.get("error")
01185:         ]
01186: 
01187:         return {
01188:             "isRange": True,
01189:             "startTime": start_result.get(
01190:                 "time"
01191:             ),
01192:             "endTime": end_result.get(
01193:                 "time"
01194:             ),
01195:             "startAmbiguous": bool(
01196:                 start_result.get("ambiguous")
01197:             ),
01198:             "endAmbiguous": bool(
01199:                 end_result.get("ambiguous")
01200:             ),
01201:             "startHour": start_result.get(
01202:                 "hour"
01203:             ),
01204:             "startMinute": (
01205:                 start_result.get("minute")
01206:             ),
01207:             "endHour": end_result.get(
01208:                 "hour"
01209:             ),
01210:             "endMinute": end_result.get(
01211:                 "minute"
01212:             ),
01213:             "meridiem": meridiem,
01214:             "errors": errors,
01215:         }
01216: 
01217:     single_result = _parse_time_segment(
01218:         normalized
01219:     )
01220: 
01221:     errors = []
01222: 
01223:     if single_result.get("error"):
01224:         errors.append(
01225:             single_result["error"]
01226:         )
01227: 
01228:     return {
01229:         "isRange": False,
01230:         "time": single_result.get("time"),
01231:         "ambiguous": bool(
01232:             single_result.get("ambiguous")
01233:         ),
01234:         "hour": single_result.get("hour"),
01235:         "minute": single_result.get("minute"),
01236:         "meridiem": meridiem,
01237:         "found": bool(
01238:             single_result.get("found")
01239:         ),
01240:         "errors": errors,
01241:     }
01242:

01248: _CONFIRM_PHRASES = {
01249:     "بله",

01251:     "اره",
01252:     "تایید",
01253:     "تأیید",
01254:     "تایید میکنم",
01255:     "تأیید میکنم",
01256:     "انجام بده",
01257:     "اوکی",
01258:     "ok",
01259:     "yes",
01260: }
01261: 
01262: _REJECT_PHRASES = {
01263:     "نه",
01264:     "خیر",
01265:     "نه ممنون",
01266:     "خیر ممنون",
01267:     "no",
01268: }
01269: 
01270: _CANCEL_CONVERSATION_PHRASES = {
01271:     "لغو",
01272:     "لغو کن",
01273:     "حذف",
01274:     "حذف کن",
01275:     "کنسل",
01276:     "کنسل کن",

01279:     "انصراف",
01280:     "منصرف شدم",
01281:     "نمیخوام",
01282:     "نمی خواهم",
01283:     "cancel",
01284: }

01288:     "غذای",
01289:     "ناهار",
01290:     "منو",
01291:     "رستوران",
01292: }
01293:

01300:     "میخوام",

01340: 
01341: def _detect_food_intent(
01342:     text: str,
01343: ) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
01344:     # FOOD_RECOMMENDATION_V3
01345:     has_food_word = _contains_any_word(
01346:         text,
01347:         _FOOD_WORDS,
01348:     )
01349: 
01350:     has_reserve_word = _contains_any_word(
01351:         text,
01352:         _RESERVE_WORDS,
01353:     )
01354: 
01355:     has_cancel_word = _contains_any_word(
01356:         text,
01357:         _CANCEL_WORDS,
01358:     )
01359: 
01360:     has_reservation_word = (
01361:         _contains_word(
01362:             text,
01363:             "رزرو",
01364:         )
01365:         or _contains_word(
01366:             text,
01367:             "رزروها",
01368:         )
01369:         or _contains_word(
01370:             text,
01371:             "رزروهای",
01372:         )
01373:     )
01374: 
01375:     has_order_word = (
01376:         _contains_word(
01377:             text,
01378:             "سفارش",
01379:         )
01380:     )
01381: 
01382:     reserve_command_words = {
01383:         "کن",
01384:         "کنم",
01385:         "بده",
01386:         "بگیر",
01387:         "بگیرم",
01388:         "بگیری",
01389:         "شه",
01390:         "شود",
01391:     }
01392: 
01393:     if (
01394:         has_cancel_word
01395:         and (
01396:             has_food_word
01397:             or has_reservation_word
01398:         )
01399:     ):
01400:         return (
01401:             "FOOD",
01402:             "CANCEL_FOOD",
01403:         )
01404: 
01405:     reservation_status_phrases = {
01406:         "چی رزرو کردم",
01407:         "چه چیزی رزرو کردم",
01408:         "چی برام رزرو شده",
01409:         "رزرو من",
01410:         "رزرو شده",
01411:         "غذای رزرو شده",
01412:     }
01413: 
01414:     if _contains_any_phrase(
01415:         text,
01416:         reservation_status_phrases,
01417:     ):
01418:         return (
01419:             "FOOD",
01420:             "SHOW_FOOD_RESERVATION",
01421:         )
01422: 
01423:     register_food_phrases = {
01424:         "ثبت غذا",
01425:         "غذا ثبت شه",
01426:         "غذا ثبت شود",
01427:         "غذا رو ثبت کن",
01428:         "غذا را ثبت کن",
01429:         "غذا انتخاب شه",
01430:         "غذا انتخاب شود",
01431:         "غذا رو انتخاب کن",
01432:         "غذا را انتخاب کن",
01433:         "برام غذا بگیر",
01434:         "برایم غذا بگیر",
01435:     }
01436: 
01437:     if (
01438:         (
01439:             (
01440:                 has_reservation_word
01441:                 or has_order_word
01442:             )
01443:             and _contains_any_word(
01444:                 text,
01445:                 reserve_command_words,
01446:             )
01447:         )
01448:         or (
01449:             has_food_word
01450:             and has_reserve_word
01451:         )
01452:         or _contains_any_phrase(
01453:             text,
01454:             register_food_phrases,
01455:         )
01456:     ):
01457:         return (
01458:             "FOOD",
01459:             "RESERVE_FOOD",
01460:         )
01461: 
01462:     menu_phrases = {
01463:         "چه غذایی",
01464:         "چه غذا",
01465:         "چی داریم",
01466:         "ناهارهای این هفته",
01467:         "منوی غذا",
01468:         "غذا رو نشون بده",
01469:         "غذا را نشان بده",
01470:         "غذای امروز رو نشون بده",
01471:         "غذای فردا رو نشون بده",
01472:     }
01473: 
01474:     show_words = set(
01475:         _SHOW_WORDS
01476:     ) | {
01477:         "نشون",
01478:         "ببین",
01479:         "بیار",
01480:     }
01481: 
01482:     if (
01483:         _contains_word(
01484:             text,
01485:             "منو",
01486:         )
01487:         or (
01488:             has_food_word
01489:             and (
01490:                 _contains_any_word(
01491:                     text,
01492:                     show_words,
01493:                 )
01494:                 or _contains_any_phrase(
01495:                     text,
01496:                     menu_phrases,
01497:                 )
01498:             )
01499:         )
01500:     ):
01501:         return (
01502:             "FOOD",
01503:             "SHOW_FOOD_MENU",
01504:         )
01505: 
01506:     if has_food_word:
01507:         return (
01508:             "FOOD",
01509:             "FOOD_UNKNOWN",
01510:         )
01511: 
01512:     return None
01513: 
01514: 
01515: def _detect_leave_intent(
01516:     text: str,
01517: ) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
01518:     if not _contains_word(
01519:         text,
01520:         "مرخصی",
01521:     ):
01522:         return None
01523: 
01524:     balance_words = {
01525:         "مانده",
01526:         "باقی",
01527:         "باقیمانده",
01528:         "موجودی",
01529:         "حساب",
01530:         "چقدر",
01531:         "دارم",
01532:         "مونده",
01533:         "ماندهام",
01534:     }
01535: 
01536:     balance_phrases = [
01537:         "چند ساعت مرخصی",
01538:         "چند روز مرخصی",
01539:         "وضعیت حساب مرخصی",
01540:         "مرخصی باقی مونده",
01541:         "مانده مرخصی",
01542:     ]
01543: 
01544:     if (
01545:         _contains_any_word(
01546:             text,
01547:             balance_words,
01548:         )
01549:         or _contains_any_phrase(
01550:             text,
01551:             balance_phrases,
01552:         )
01553:     ):
01554:         return (
01555:             "LEAVE",
01556:             "GET_LEAVE_BALANCE",
01557:         )
01558: 
01559:     if _contains_any_word(
01560:         text,
01561:         _CANCEL_WORDS,
01562:     ):
01563:         return (
01564:             "LEAVE",
01565:             "CANCEL_LEAVE",
01566:         )
01567: 
01568:     if _contains_any_word(
01569:         text,
01570:         _CREATE_WORDS,
01571:     ):
01572:         return (
01573:             "LEAVE",
01574:             "CREATE_LEAVE",
01575:         )
01576: 
01577:     if _contains_any_word(
01578:         text,
01579:         _SHOW_WORDS,
01580:     ):
01581:         return (
01582:             "LEAVE",
01583:             "SHOW_LEAVE_REQUESTS",
01584:         )
01585: 
01586:     return "LEAVE", "LEAVE_UNKNOWN"
01587: 
01588: 
01589: def _detect_mission_intent(
01590:     text: str,
01591: ) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
01592:     if not _contains_word(
01593:         text,
01594:         "ماموریت",
01595:     ):
01596:         return None
01597: 
01598:     if _contains_any_word(
01599:         text,
01600:         _CANCEL_WORDS,
01601:     ):
01602:         return (
01603:             "MISSION",
01604:             "CANCEL_MISSION",
01605:         )
01606: 
01607:     if _contains_any_word(
01608:         text,
01609:         _CREATE_WORDS,
01610:     ):
01611:         return (
01612:             "MISSION",
01613:             "CREATE_MISSION",
01614:         )
01615: 
01616:     if _contains_any_word(
01617:         text,
01618:         _SHOW_WORDS,
01619:     ):
01620:         return (
01621:             "MISSION",
01622:             "SHOW_MISSIONS",
01623:         )
01624: 
01625:     return "MISSION", "MISSION_UNKNOWN"
01626: 
01627: 
01628: def _detect_attendance_intent(
01629:     text: str,
01630:     time_entities: dict[str, Any],
01631: ) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
01632:     attendance_words = {
01633:         "تردد",
01634:         "ورود",
01635:         "خروج",
01636:         "ساعتزنی",
01637:     }
01638: 
01639:     if not _contains_any_word(
01640:         text,
01641:         attendance_words,
01642:     ):
01643:         return None
01644: 
01645:     show_phrases = [
01646:         "نشان بده",
01647:         "نمایش بده",
01648:         "تردد من",
01649:         "سوابق تردد",
01650:         "گزارش تردد",
01651:     ]
01652: 
01653:     if _contains_any_phrase(
01654:         text,
01655:         show_phrases,
01656:     ):
01657:         return (
01658:             "ATTENDANCE",
01659:             "SHOW_ATTENDANCE",
01660:         )
01661: 
01662:     if (
01663:         time_entities.get("isRange")
01664:         and (
01665:             time_entities.get("startTime")
01666:             or time_entities.get("endTime")
01667:         )
01668:     ):
01669:         return (
01670:             "ATTENDANCE",
01671:             "CREATE_TIME_EVENT_RANGE",
01672:         )
01673: 
01674:     if (
01675:         _contains_any_word(
01676:             text,
01677:             _CREATE_WORDS,
01678:         )
01679:         or _contains_word(text, "ورود")
01680:         or _contains_word(text, "خروج")
01681:     ):
01682:         return (
01683:             "ATTENDANCE",
01684:             "CREATE_TIME_EVENT",
01685:         )
01686: 
01687:     return (
01688:         "ATTENDANCE",
01689:         "SHOW_ATTENDANCE",
01690:     )
01691: 
01692: 
01693: def _detect_domain_intent(
01694:     text: str,
01695:     time_entities: dict[str, Any],
01696: ) -> tuple[str, str]:
01697:     food_result = _detect_food_intent(
01698:         text
01699:     )
01700: 
01701:     if food_result:
01702:         return food_result
01703: 
01704:     leave_result = _detect_leave_intent(
01705:         text
01706:     )
01707: 
01708:     if leave_result:
01709:         return leave_result
01710: 
01711:     mission_result = _detect_mission_intent(
01712:         text
01713:     )
01714: 
01715:     if mission_result:
01716:         return mission_result
01717: 
01718:     attendance_result = (
01719:         _detect_attendance_intent(
01720:             text,
01721:             time_entities,
01722:         )
01723:     )
01724: 
01725:     if attendance_result:
01726:         return attendance_result
01727: 
01728:     if _contains_any_word(
01729:         text,
01730:         _OUT_OF_SCOPE_WORDS,
01731:     ):
01732:         return (
01733:             "OUT_OF_SCOPE",
01734:             "UNSUPPORTED",
01735:         )
01736: 
01737:     if _contains_any_word(
01738:         text,
01739:         _CREATE_WORDS,
01740:     ):
01741:         return (
01742:             "UNKNOWN",
01743:             "NEEDS_DOMAIN",
01744:         )
01745: 
01746:     return (
01747:         "OUT_OF_SCOPE",
01748:         "UNSUPPORTED",
01749:     )
01750: 
01751: 
01752: # ============================================================
01753: # Entities, missing fields and guidance
01754: # ============================================================
01755: 
01756: def _extract_event_type(
01757:     text: str,
01758: ) -> str | None:
01759:     has_entry = _contains_word(
01760:         text,
01761:         "ورود",
01762:     )
01763: 
01764:     has_exit = _contains_word(
01765:         text,
01766:         "خروج",
01767:     )
01768: 
01769:     if has_entry and not has_exit:
01770:         return "ENTRY"
01771: 
01772:     if has_exit and not has_entry:
01773:         return "EXIT"
01774: 
01775:     return None
01776: 
01777: 
01778: def _build_entities(
01779:     *,
01780:     text: str,
01781:     domain: str,
01782:     intent: str,
01783:     reference_date: str,
01784:     previous_entities: dict[str, Any],
01785:     time_entities: dict[str, Any],
01786: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
01787:     entities = dict(previous_entities)
01788: 
01789:     detected_date = _extract_date(
01790:         text,
01791:         reference_date,
01792:     )
01793: 
01794:     if detected_date:
01795:         entities["date"] = detected_date
01796: 
01797:     if _contains_phrase(
01798:         text,
01799:         "این هفته",
01800:     ):
01801:         entities["dateScope"] = (
01802:             "CURRENT_WEEK"
01803:         )
01804: 
01805:     if _contains_phrase(
01806:         text,
01807:         "هفته بعد",
01808:     ):
01809:         entities["dateScope"] = (
01810:             "NEXT_WEEK"
01811:         )
01812: 
01813:     if domain in {
01814:         "LEAVE",
01815:         "MISSION",
01816:     }:
01817:         if detected_date:
01818:             entities["startDate"] = (
01819:                 detected_date
01820:             )
01821:             entities["endDate"] = (
01822:                 detected_date
01823:             )
01824: 
01825:     if domain == "ATTENDANCE":
01826:         event_type = _extract_event_type(
01827:             text
01828:         )
01829: 
01830:         if event_type:
01831:             entities["eventType"] = (
01832:                 event_type
01833:             )
01834: 
01835:         meridiem = time_entities.get(
01836:             "meridiem"
01837:         )
01838: 
01839:         if time_entities.get("isRange"):
01840:             start_time = time_entities.get(
01841:                 "startTime"
01842:             )
01843:             end_time = time_entities.get(
01844:                 "endTime"
01845:             )
01846: 
01847:             if start_time:
01848:                 entities["startTime"] = (
01849:                     start_time
01850:                 )
01851:                 entities.pop(
01852:                     "ambiguousStartHour",
01853:                     None,
01854:                 )
01855:                 entities.pop(
01856:                     "ambiguousStartMinute",
01857:                     None,
01858:                 )
01859: 
01860:             elif (
01861:                 time_entities.get(
01862:                     "startAmbiguous"
01863:                 )
01864:                 and time_entities.get(
01865:                     "startHour"
01866:                 )
01867:                 is not None
01868:             ):
01869:                 entities[
01870:                     "ambiguousStartHour"
01871:                 ] = int(
01872:                     time_entities["startHour"]
01873:                 )
01874:                 entities[
01875:                     "ambiguousStartMinute"
01876:                 ] = int(
01877:                     time_entities.get(
01878:                         "startMinute"
01879:                     )
01880:                     or 0
01881:                 )
01882: 
01883:             if end_time:
01884:                 entities["endTime"] = (
01885:                     end_time
01886:                 )
01887:                 entities.pop(
01888:                     "ambiguousEndHour",
01889:                     None,
01890:                 )
01891:                 entities.pop(
01892:                     "ambiguousEndMinute",
01893:                     None,
01894:                 )
01895: 
01896:             elif (
01897:                 time_entities.get(
01898:                     "endAmbiguous"
01899:                 )
01900:                 and time_entities.get(
01901:                     "endHour"
01902:                 )
01903:                 is not None
01904:             ):
01905:                 entities[
01906:                     "ambiguousEndHour"
01907:                 ] = int(
01908:                     time_entities["endHour"]
01909:                 )
01910:                 entities[
01911:                     "ambiguousEndMinute"
01912:                 ] = int(
01913:                     time_entities.get(
01914:                         "endMinute"
01915:                     )
01916:                     or 0
01917:                 )
01918: 
01919:             if meridiem:
01920:                 if (
01921:                     not entities.get(
01922:                         "startTime"
01923:                     )
01924:                     and entities.get(
01925:                         "ambiguousStartHour"
01926:                     )
01927:                     is not None
01928:                 ):
01929:                     entities["startTime"] = (
01930:                         _format_time(
01931:                             _apply_meridiem(
01932:                                 int(
01933:                                     entities[
01934:                                         "ambiguousStartHour"
01935:                                     ]
01936:                                 ),
01937:                                 str(meridiem),
01938:                             ),
01939:                             int(
01940:                                 entities.get(
01941:                                     "ambiguousStartMinute"
01942:                                 )
01943:                                 or 0
01944:                             ),
01945:                         )
01946:                     )
01947:                     entities.pop(
01948:                         "ambiguousStartHour",
01949:                         None,
01950:                     )
01951:                     entities.pop(
01952:                         "ambiguousStartMinute",
01953:                         None,
01954:                     )
01955: 
01956:                 if (
01957:                     not entities.get(
01958:                         "endTime"
01959:                     )
01960:                     and entities.get(
01961:                         "ambiguousEndHour"
01962:                     )
01963:                     is not None
01964:                 ):
01965:                     entities["endTime"] = (
01966:                         _format_time(
01967:                             _apply_meridiem(
01968:                                 int(
01969:                                     entities[
01970:                                         "ambiguousEndHour"
01971:                                     ]
01972:                                 ),
01973:                                 str(meridiem),
01974:                             ),
01975:                             int(
01976:                                 entities.get(
01977:                                     "ambiguousEndMinute"
01978:                                 )
01979:                                 or 0
01980:                             ),
01981:                         )
01982:                     )
01983:                     entities.pop(
01984:                         "ambiguousEndHour",
01985:                         None,
01986:                     )
01987:                     entities.pop(
01988:                         "ambiguousEndMinute",
01989:                         None,
01990:                     )
01991: 
01992:         else:
01993:             time_value = time_entities.get(
01994:                 "time"
01995:             )
01996: 
01997:             if time_value:
01998:                 entities["time"] = time_value
01999:                 entities.pop(
02000:                     "ambiguousHour",
02001:                     None,
02002:                 )
02003:                 entities.pop(
02004:                     "ambiguousMinute",
02005:                     None,
02006:                 )
02007: 
02008:             elif (
02009:                 time_entities.get("ambiguous")
02010:                 and time_entities.get("hour")
02011:                 is not None
02012:             ):
02013:                 entities["ambiguousHour"] = (
02014:                     int(time_entities["hour"])
02015:                 )
02016:                 entities["ambiguousMinute"] = (
02017:                     int(
02018:                         time_entities.get(
02019:                             "minute"
02020:                         )
02021:                         or 0
02022:                     )
02023:                 )
02024: 
02025:             elif (
02026:                 meridiem
02027:                 and entities.get(
02028:                     "ambiguousHour"
02029:                 )
02030:                 is not None
02031:             ):
02032:                 entities["time"] = _format_time(
02033:                     _apply_meridiem(
02034:                         int(
02035:                             entities[
02036:                                 "ambiguousHour"
02037:                             ]
02038:                         ),
02039:                         str(meridiem),
02040:                     ),
02041:                     int(
02042:                         entities.get(
02043:                             "ambiguousMinute"
02044:                         )
02045:                         or 0
02046:                     ),
02047:                 )
02048:                 entities.pop(
02049:                     "ambiguousHour",
02050:                     None,
02051:                 )
02052:                 entities.pop(
02053:                     "ambiguousMinute",
02054:                     None,
02055:                 )
02056: 
02057:     return entities
02058: 
02059: 
02060: def _required_fields(
02061:     domain: str,
02062:     intent: str,
02063:     entities: dict[str, Any],
02064:     time_entities: dict[str, Any],
02065: ) -> list[str]:
02066:     missing: list[str] = []
02067: 
02068:     if intent == "RESERVE_FOOD":
02069:         if not (
02070:             entities.get("date")
02071:             or entities.get("dateScope")
02072:         ):
02073:             missing.append("date")
02074: 
02075:     elif intent in {
02076:         "CREATE_LEAVE",
02077:         "CREATE_MISSION",
02078:     }:
02079:         if not entities.get("startDate"):
02080:             missing.append("date")
02081: 
02082:     elif intent == "CREATE_TIME_EVENT":
02083:         if not entities.get("date"):
02084:             missing.append("date")
02085: 
02086:         if (
02087:             time_entities.get("ambiguous")
02088:             or entities.get(
02089:                 "ambiguousHour"
02090:             )
02091:             is not None
02092:         ):
02093:             missing.append("meridiem")
02094: 
02095:         elif not entities.get("time"):
02096:             missing.append("time")
02097: 
02098:         if not entities.get("eventType"):
02099:             missing.append("eventType")
02100: 
02101:     elif intent == "CREATE_TIME_EVENT_RANGE":
02102:         if not entities.get("date"):
02103:             missing.append("date")
02104: 
02105:         if (
02106:             time_entities.get(
02107:                 "startAmbiguous"
02108:             )
02109:             or time_entities.get(
02110:                 "endAmbiguous"
02111:             )
02112:             or entities.get(
02113:                 "ambiguousStartHour"
02114:             )
02115:             is not None
02116:             or entities.get(
02117:                 "ambiguousEndHour"
02118:             )
02119:             is not None
02120:         ):
02121:             missing.append("meridiem")
02122: 
02123:         if not entities.get("startTime"):
02124:             missing.append("startTime")
02125: 
02126:         if not entities.get("endTime"):
02127:             missing.append("endTime")
02128: 
02129:     elif intent in {
02130:         "FOOD_UNKNOWN",
02131:         "LEAVE_UNKNOWN",
02132:         "MISSION_UNKNOWN",
02133:         "NEEDS_DOMAIN",
02134:     }:
02135:         missing.append("intent")
02136: 
02137:     return list(dict.fromkeys(missing))
02138: 
02139: 
02140: def _clarification_message(
02141:     *,
02142:     domain: str,
02143:     intent: str,
02144:     missing_fields: list[str],
02145:     errors: list[str],
02146: ) -> str | None:
02147:     if errors:
02148:         return (
02149:             f"{errors[0]} "
02150:             "برای نمونه بنویسید: "
02151:             "ساعت ۱۰ و ۵۰ دقیقه."
02152:         )
02153: 
02154:     if "meridiem" in missing_fields:
02155:         return (
02156:             "منظورتان صبح است یا شب؟ "
02157:             "برای نمونه: ساعت ۱۰ صبح "
02158:             "یا ساعت ۱۰ شب."
02159:         )
02160: 
02161:     if "date" in missing_fields:
02162:         if domain == "FOOD":
02163:             return (
02164:                 "برای چه روزی غذا رزرو شود؟ "
02165:                 "مثلاً امروز، فردا یا کل هفته."
02166:             )
02167: 
02168:         return (
02169:             "تاریخ درخواست را بفرمایید؛ "
02170:             "مثلاً امروز، فردا یا "
02171:             "۱۴۰۵/۰۴/۲۵."
02172:         )
02173: 
02174:     if "time" in missing_fields:
02175:         return (
02176:             "ساعت تردد را بفرمایید؛ "
02177:             "مثلاً ساعت ۸ صبح یا "
02178:             "۱۰ و ۵۰ دقیقه شب."
02179:         )
02180: 
02181:     if "eventType" in missing_fields:
02182:         return (
02183:             "این تردد مربوط به ورود است "
02184:             "یا خروج؟"
02185:         )
02186: 
02187:     if "startTime" in missing_fields:
02188:         return (
02189:             "ساعت شروع تردد را بفرمایید."
02190:         )
02191: 
02192:     if "endTime" in missing_fields:
02193:         return (
02194:             "ساعت پایان تردد را بفرمایید."
02195:         )
02196: 
02197:     if "intent" in missing_fields:
02198:         if domain == "FOOD":
02199:             return (
02200:                 "موضوع غذا را متوجه شدم. "
02201:                 "می‌خواهید منو را ببینید، "
02202:                 "غذا رزرو کنید یا رزرو را "
02203:                 "لغو کنید؟"
02204:             )
02205: 
02206:         if domain == "LEAVE":
02207:             return (
02208:                 "موضوع مرخصی را متوجه شدم. "
02209:                 "می‌خواهید مانده مرخصی را "
02210:                 "ببینید یا درخواست جدید "
02211:                 "ثبت کنید؟"
02212:             )
02213: 
02214:         return (
02215:             "لطفاً مشخص کنید چه کاری باید "
02216:             "انجام شود؛ مثلاً رزرو غذا، "
02217:             "ثبت مرخصی یا ثبت تردد."
02218:         )
02219: 
02220:     if intent == "EMPTY_MESSAGE":
02221:         return (
02222:             "پیام شما خالی است. "
02223:             "برای نمونه بنویسید: "
02224:             "مانده مرخصی من چقدره؟"
02225:         )
02226: 
02227:     return None
02228: 
02229: 
02230: def _rejection_message(
02231:     domain: str,
02232:     parent_intent: str | None,
02233: ) -> str:
02234:     if domain == "FOOD":
02235:         return (
02236:             "باشه، پیشنهاد قبلی را انجام نمی‌دهم. "
02237:             "روز یا غذای دیگری را بگویید."
02238:         )
02239: 
02240:     if domain == "LEAVE":
02241:         return (
02242:             "باشه، درخواست قبلی ثبت نمی‌شود. "
02243:             "تاریخ یا نوع درخواست دیگری را بگویید."
02244:         )
02245: 
02246:     if domain == "MISSION":
02247:         return (
02248:             "باشه، مأموریت قبلی ثبت نمی‌شود. "
02249:             "جزئیات جدید را بگویید."
02250:         )
02251: 
02252:     if domain == "ATTENDANCE":
02253:         return (
02254:             "باشه، تردد قبلی ثبت نمی‌شود. "
02255:             "تاریخ، ساعت یا نوع تردد جدید را بگویید."
02256:         )
02257: 
02258:     if parent_intent:
02259:         return (
02260:             "باشه، پیشنهاد قبلی انجام نمی‌شود. "
02261:             "درخواست جدیدتان را بگویید."
02262:         )
02263: 
02264:     return (
02265:         "باشه، آن مورد انجام نمی‌شود. "
02266:         "درخواست جدیدتان را بگویید."
02267:     )
02268: 
02269: 
02270: def _confidence_for(
02271:     domain: str,
02272:     intent: str,
02273: ) -> float:
02274:     if intent in {
02275:         "UNSUPPORTED",
02276:         "EMPTY_MESSAGE",
02277:     }:
02278:         return 0.99
02279: 
02280:     if intent in {
02281:         "FOOD_UNKNOWN",
02282:         "LEAVE_UNKNOWN",
02283:         "MISSION_UNKNOWN",
02284:         "NEEDS_DOMAIN",
02285:     }:
02286:         return 0.65
02287: 
02288:     if domain != "UNKNOWN":
02289:         return 0.95
02290: 
02291:     return 0.50
02292: 
02293: 
02294: def _is_read_only_intent(
02295:     intent: str,
02296: ) -> bool:
02297:     return intent in {
02298:         "GET_LEAVE_BALANCE",
02299:         "SHOW_LEAVE_REQUESTS",
02300:         "SHOW_MISSIONS",
02301:         "SHOW_ATTENDANCE",
02302:         "SHOW_FOOD_MENU",
02303:         "SHOW_FOOD_RESERVATION",
02304:         "UNSUPPORTED",
02305:     }
02306: 
02307: 
02308: def _build_state(
02309:     *,
02310:     domain: str,
02311:     intent: str,
02312:     entities: dict[str, Any],
02313:     missing_fields: list[str],
02314: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
02315:     if intent in {
02316:         "UNSUPPORTED",
02317:         "EMPTY_MESSAGE",
02318:         "CANCEL_CONVERSATION",
02319:     }:
02320:         return {}
02321: 
02322:     return {
02323:         "domain": domain,
02324:         "intent": intent,
02325:         "entities": dict(entities),
02326:         "missingFields": list(
02327:             missing_fields
02328:         ),
02329:     }
02330: 
02331: 
02332: # ============================================================
02333: # Public API
02334: # ============================================================
02335: 
02336: def analyze_message(
02337:     message: str,
02338:     *,
02339:     reference_date: str,
02340:     conversation_state: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
02341: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
02342:     text = normalize_text(message)
02343:     previous_state = (
02344:         dict(conversation_state)
02345:         if isinstance(
02346:             conversation_state,
02347:             dict,
02348:         )
02349:         else {}
02350:     )
02351: 
02352:     if not text:
02353:         return {
02354:             "domain": "UNKNOWN",
02355:             "intent": "EMPTY_MESSAGE",
02356:             "entities": {},
02357:             "confidence": 1.0,
02358:             "missingFields": [
02359:                 "message"
02360:             ],
02361:             "needsClarification": True,
02362:             "conversationComplete": False,
02363:             "clarification": (
02364:                 "پیام شما خالی است. "
02365:                 "لطفاً درخواستتان را بنویسید."
02366:             ),
02367:             "errors": [],
02368:             "state": {},
02369:         }
02370: 
02371:     # --------------------------------------------------------
02372:     # Explicit conversation cancellation
02373:     # --------------------------------------------------------
02374: 
02375:     if previous_state:
02376:         normalized_cancel_values = {
02377:             normalize_text(value)
02378:             for value in (
02379:                 _CANCEL_CONVERSATION_PHRASES
02380:             )
02381:         }
02382: 
02383:         has_domain_word = (
02384:             _contains_any_word(
02385:                 text,
02386:                 _FOOD_WORDS,
02387:             )
02388:             or _contains_word(
02389:                 text,
02390:                 "مرخصی",
02391:             )
02392:             or _contains_word(
02393:                 text,
02394:                 "ماموریت",
02395:             )
02396:             or _contains_any_word(
02397:                 text,
02398:                 {
02399:                     "تردد",
02400:                     "ورود",
02401:                     "خروج",
02402:                 },
02403:             )
02404:         )
02405: 
02406:         if (
02407:             text in normalized_cancel_values
02408:             or (
02409:                 _contains_word(
02410:                     text,
02411:                     "بیخیال",
02412:                 )
02413:                 and not has_domain_word
02414:             )
02415:         ):
02416:             return {
02417:                 "domain": (
02418:                     previous_state.get(
02419:                         "domain"
02420:                     )
02421:                     or "UNKNOWN"
02422:                 ),
02423:                 "intent": (
02424:                     "CANCEL_CONVERSATION"
02425:                 ),
02426:                 "entities": {},
02427:                 "confidence": 1.0,
02428:                 "missingFields": [],
02429:                 "needsClarification": False,
02430:                 "conversationComplete": True,
02431:                 "clarification": None,
02432:                 "errors": [],
02433:                 "state": {},
02434:             }
02435: 
02436:     # --------------------------------------------------------
02437:     # Explicit rejection without ending the conversation
02438:     # --------------------------------------------------------
02439: 
02440:     if previous_state:
02441:         normalized_reject_values = {
02442:             normalize_text(value)
02443:             for value in _REJECT_PHRASES
02444:         }
02445: 
02446:         if text in normalized_reject_values:
02447:             previous_domain_value = str(
02448:                 previous_state.get(
02449:                     "domain"
02450:                 )
02451:                 or "UNKNOWN"
02452:             )
02453: 
02454:             previous_intent_value = (
02455:                 previous_state.get(
02456:                     "intent"
02457:                 )
02458:             )
02459: 
02460:             previous_entities_value = (
02461:                 previous_state.get(
02462:                     "entities"
02463:                 )
02464:                 if isinstance(
02465:                     previous_state.get(
02466:                         "entities"
02467:                     ),
02468:                     dict,
02469:                 )
02470:                 else {}
02471:             )
02472: 
02473:             rejected_state = {
02474:                 "domain": (
02475:                     previous_domain_value
02476:                 ),
02477:                 "intent": (
02478:                     str(
02479:                         previous_intent_value
02480:                         or "NEEDS_DOMAIN"
02481:                     )
02482:                 ),
02483:                 "entities": dict(
02484:                     previous_entities_value
02485:                 ),
02486:                 "missingFields": [
02487:                     "alternative"
02488:                 ],
02489:                 "proposalRejected": True,
02490:             }
02491: 
02492:             return {
02493:                 "domain": (
02494:                     previous_domain_value
02495:                 ),
02496:                 "intent": "REJECT",
02497:                 "entities": {
02498:                     **dict(
02499:                         previous_entities_value
02500:                     ),
02501:                     "parentIntent": (
02502:                         previous_intent_value
02503:                     ),
02504:                 },
02505:                 "confidence": 1.0,
02506:                 "missingFields": [
02507:                     "alternative"
02508:                 ],
02509:                 "needsClarification": True,
02510:                 "conversationComplete": False,
02511:                 "clarification": (
02512:                     _rejection_message(
02513:                         previous_domain_value,
02514:                         (
02515:                             str(
02516:                                 previous_intent_value
02517:                             )
02518:                             if previous_intent_value
02519:                             else None
02520:                         ),
02521:                     )
02522:                 ),
02523:                 "errors": [],
02524:                 "state": rejected_state,
02525:             }
02526: 
02527:     # --------------------------------------------------------
02528:     # Explicit confirmation
02529:     # --------------------------------------------------------
02530: 
02531:     normalized_confirm_values = {
02532:         normalize_text(value)
02533:         for value in _CONFIRM_PHRASES
02534:     }
02535: 
02536:     if (
02537:         previous_state
02538:         and text in normalized_confirm_values
02539:     ):
02540:         if previous_state.get(
02541:             "proposalRejected"
02542:         ):
02543:             previous_domain_value = str(
02544:                 previous_state.get(
02545:                     "domain"
02546:                 )
02547:                 or "UNKNOWN"
02548:             )
02549: 
02550:             return {
02551:                 "domain": (
02552:                     previous_domain_value
02553:                 ),
02554:                 "intent": "REJECT",
02555:                 "entities": dict(
02556:                     previous_state.get(
02557:                         "entities"
02558:                     )
02559:                     or {}
02560:                 ),
02561:                 "confidence": 1.0,
02562:                 "missingFields": [
02563:                     "alternative"
02564:                 ],
02565:                 "needsClarification": True,
02566:                 "conversationComplete": False,
02567:                 "clarification": (
02568:                     "پیشنهاد قبلی رد شده است. "
02569:                     "لطفاً گزینه یا جزئیات جدید "
02570:                     "را بگویید."
02571:                 ),
02572:                 "errors": [],
02573:                 "state": previous_state,
02574:             }
02575: 
02576:         parent_intent = (
02577:             previous_state.get("intent")
02578:         )
02579: 
02580:         parent_entities = (
02581:             previous_state.get("entities")
02582:             if isinstance(
02583:                 previous_state.get(
02584:                     "entities"
02585:                 ),
02586:                 dict,
02587:             )
02588:             else {}
02589:         )
02590: 
02591:         entities = dict(parent_entities)
02592:         entities["parentIntent"] = (
02593:             parent_intent
02594:         )
02595: 
02596:         return {
02597:             "domain": (
02598:                 previous_state.get(
02599:                     "domain"
02600:                 )
02601:                 or "UNKNOWN"
02602:             ),
02603:             "intent": "CONFIRM",
02604:             "entities": entities,
02605:             "confidence": 1.0,
02606:             "missingFields": [],
02607:             "needsClarification": False,
02608:             "conversationComplete": True,
02609:             "clarification": None,
02610:             "errors": [],
02611:             "state": previous_state,
02612:         }
02613: 
02614:     time_entities = (
02615:         _extract_time_entities(text)
02616:     )
02617: 
02618:     detected_domain, detected_intent = (
02619:         _detect_domain_intent(
02620:             text,
02621:             time_entities,
02622:         )
02623:     )
02624: 
02625:     previous_domain = (
02626:         previous_state.get("domain")
02627:     )
02628: 
02629:     previous_intent = (
02630:         previous_state.get("intent")
02631:     )
02632: 
02633:     current_has_known_domain = (
02634:         detected_domain
02635:         not in {
02636:             "UNKNOWN",
02637:             "OUT_OF_SCOPE",
02638:         }
02639:     )
02640: 
02641:     has_new_entities = bool(
02642:         _extract_date(
02643:             text,
02644:             reference_date,
02645:         )
02646:         or time_entities.get("time")
02647:         or time_entities.get("startTime")
02648:         or time_entities.get("endTime")
02649:         or _extract_event_type(text)
02650:         or _extract_meridiem(text)
02651:         or _contains_phrase(
02652:             text,
02653:             "این هفته",
02654:         )
02655:         or _contains_phrase(
02656:             text,
02657:             "هفته بعد",
02658:         )
02659:     )
02660: 
02661:     # Continue the previous conversation when the new
02662:     # message only supplies missing information.
02663:     if (
02664:         previous_state
02665:         and not current_has_known_domain
02666:         and has_new_entities
02667:     ):
02668:         domain = (
02669:             str(previous_domain)
02670:             if previous_domain
02671:             else "UNKNOWN"
02672:         )
02673: 
02674:         intent = (
02675:             str(previous_intent)
02676:             if previous_intent
02677:             else "NEEDS_DOMAIN"
02678:         )
02679: 
02680:     else:
02681:         domain = detected_domain
02682:         intent = detected_intent
02683: 
02684:     previous_entities = (
02685:         previous_state.get("entities")
02686:         if (
02687:             previous_state
02688:             and isinstance(
02689:                 previous_state.get(
02690:                     "entities"
02691:                 ),
02692:                 dict,
02693:             )
02694:             and domain
02695:             == previous_state.get(
02696:                 "domain"
02697:             )
02698:         )
02699:         else {}
02700:     )
02701: 
02702:     entities = _build_entities(
02703:         text=text,
02704:         domain=domain,
02705:         intent=intent,
02706:         reference_date=reference_date,
02707:         previous_entities=(
02708:             previous_entities
02709:         ),
02710:         time_entities=time_entities,
02711:     )
02712: 
02713:     # A corrective message such as:
02714:     # "نه، ساعت ده و پنجاه دقیقه شب"
02715:     # must replace the previous time.
02716:     if (
02717:         previous_state
02718:         and has_new_entities
02719:         and _contains_word(text, "نه")
02720:     ):
02721:         previous_intent_value = (
02722:             previous_state.get("intent")
02723:         )
02724: 
02725:         if previous_intent_value:
02726:             intent = str(
02727:                 previous_intent_value
02728:             )
02729: 
02730:         previous_domain_value = (
02731:             previous_state.get("domain")
02732:         )
02733: 
02734:         if previous_domain_value:
02735:             domain = str(
02736:                 previous_domain_value
02737:             )
02738: 
02739:     errors = list(
02740:         time_entities.get("errors")
02741:         or []
02742:     )
02743: 
02744:     missing_fields = _required_fields(
02745:         domain,
02746:         intent,
02747:         entities,
02748:         time_entities,
02749:     )
02750: 
02751:     if errors and "time" not in missing_fields:
02752:         missing_fields.append("time")
02753: 
02754:     needs_clarification = bool(
02755:         missing_fields
02756:         or errors
02757:     )
02758: 
02759:     clarification = (
02760:         _clarification_message(
02761:             domain=domain,
02762:             intent=intent,
02763:             missing_fields=missing_fields,
02764:             errors=errors,
02765:         )
02766:     )
02767: 
02768:     if intent == "UNSUPPORTED":
02769:         clarification = (
02770:             "این درخواست خارج از حوزه خدمات "
02771:             "فعلی من است. من می‌توانم در امور "
02772:             "غذا، مرخصی، مأموریت و تردد "
02773:             "کمکتان کنم."
02774:         )
02775: 
02776:     if intent == "NEEDS_DOMAIN":
02777:         clarification = (
02778:             "چه چیزی ثبت شود؟ "
02779:             "برای نمونه بنویسید: "
02780:             "رزرو غذا برای فردا، "
02781:             "ثبت مرخصی یا ثبت تردد."
02782:         )
02783: 
02784:     conversation_complete = (
02785:         not needs_clarification
02786:         and _is_read_only_intent(
02787:             intent
02788:         )
02789:     )
02790: 
02791:     if intent == "UNSUPPORTED":
02792:         conversation_complete = True
02793: 
02794:     state = _build_state(
02795:         domain=domain,
02796:         intent=intent,
02797:         entities=entities,
02798:         missing_fields=missing_fields,
02799:     )
02800: 
02801:     return {
02802:         "domain": domain,
02803:         "intent": intent,
02804:         "entities": entities,
02805:         "confidence": _confidence_for(
02806:             domain,
02807:             intent,
02808:         ),
02809:         "missingFields": (
02810:             missing_fields
02811:         ),
02812:         "needsClarification": (
02813:             needs_clarification
02814:         ),
02815:         "conversationComplete": (
02816:             conversation_complete
02817:         ),
02818:         "clarification": clarification,
02819:         "errors": errors,
02820:         "state": state,
02821:     }
02822: 
02823: 
02824: __all__ = [
02825:     "analyze_message",
02826:     "normalize_text",
02827: ]

00261:

00263:             completed = await asyncio.to_thread(
00264:                 self.repository.complete_pending_action,
00265:                 action_id,
00266:                 "succeeded",
00267:                 result=result,
00268:             )
00269: 
00270:         except Exception as exc:
00271:             logger.critical(
00272:                 "Action completion persistence failed action_id=%s",

00300:             ) from exc

00302:         if not completed:
00303:             persisted_state = await asyncio.to_thread(
00304:                 self.repository.get_action_state,
00305:                 action_id,
00306:                 employee_id,
00307:             )
00308:

00310:                 persisted_state

00313:                 return persisted_state

00323:                 (
00324:                     "عملیات اجرا شد اما وضعیت نهایی نامطمئن است. "
00325:                     "عملیات را دوباره اجرا نکنید."
00326:                 ),
00327:                 status_code=500,
00328:                 code="ACTION_RESULT_UNKNOWN",
00329:                 details={
00330:                     "actionId": action_id,
00331:                 },
00332:             )
00333: 
00334:         return {

00336:             "actionType": record.action_type,
00337:             "status": "succeeded",

00498:                 ),
00499:             },
00500:             "unknown": {
00501:                 "reasonCode": "ACTION_RESULT_UNKNOWN",
00502:                 "message": (

00504:                     "تکراری، امکان لغو آن وجود ندارد و باید وضعیت آن بررسی شود."
00505:                 ),
00506:             },
00507:         }
00508: 
00509:         guidance = status_guidance.get(
00510:             current.status,
00511:             {
00512:                 "reasonCode": "ACTION_NOT_CANCELLABLE",
00513:                 "message": (
00514:                     f"این عملیات در وضعیت «{current.status}» قرار دارد "

00517:             },

00522:             "actionType": current.action_type,

00532:     async def _execute(
00533:         self,
00534:         context: AgentContext,
00535:         action_type: str,
00536:         payload: dict[str, Any],
00537:         idempotency_key: str,
00538:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
00539:         if action_type == "RESERVE_FOOD":

00542:                 payload,

00548:                 idempotency_key=idempotency_key,

00554:                 payload,

00563:         if action_type == "RATE_FOOD":
00564:             return await self.food.set_rating(
00565:                 context,
00566:                 reserve_id=payload["reserveId"],
00567:                 rate=payload["rate"],
00568:                 comment=payload.get("comment") or "",
00569:                 is_anonymous=payload.get(
00570:                     "isAnonymous",
00571:                     False,
00572:                 ),
00573:                 idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
00574:             )
00575: 
00576:         if action_type == "DELETE_LEAVE":
00577:             return await self.hr.delete_leave_request(
00578:                 context,
00579:                 str(payload["requestID"]),
00580:                 payload.get("changeStateID") or 0,
00581:                 str(payload["leaveKey"]),
00582:                 idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
00583:             )
00584: 
00585:         if action_type == "CREATE_TIME_EVENT":
00586:             events = await self.hr.get_time_events(
00587:                 context,
00588:                 payload["eventDate"],
00589:                 payload["eventDate"],
00590:             )

00592:             valid, existing = (
00593:                 self.hr.validate_time_event_distance(
00594:                     events.get("data") or [],
00595:                     payload["eventTime"],
00596:                 )
00597:             )
00598: 
00599:             if not valid:

00602:                     details={
00603:                         "existingTime": existing,
00604:                     },

00610:                 payload["eventTime"],

00617:                 context

00626:                         item.get("absenceTypeCode")

00656:                     payload.get("startTime") or ""

00659:                     payload.get("endTime") or ""

00692:     @staticmethod
00693:     def _food_context_from_payload(
00694:         context: AgentContext,
00695:         payload: dict[str, Any],
00696:     ) -> AgentContext:
00697:         restaurant_id = parse_int(
00698:             payload.get("restaurantId")
00699:         )
00700: 
00701:         if restaurant_id is None:
00702:             restaurant_id = parse_int(
00703:                 getattr(
00704:                     context,
00705:                     "restaurant_id",
00706:                     None,
00707:                 )
00708:             )
00709: 
00710:         meal_id = parse_int(
00711:             payload.get("mealId")
00712:         )
00713: 
00714:         if meal_id is None:
00715:             meal_id = parse_int(
00716:                 getattr(
00717:                     context,
00718:                     "meal_id",
00719:                     None,
00720:                 )
00721:             )
00722: 
00723:         if meal_id is None:
00724:             meal_id = DEFAULT_FOOD_MEAL_ID
00725: 
00726:         if (
00727:             restaurant_id is None
00728:             or restaurant_id <= 0
00729:         ):
00730:             raise AppError(
00731:                 (
00732:                     "رستوران عملیات غذا مشخص نیست. "
00733:                     "درخواست غذا را دوباره از چت شروع کنید."
00734:                 ),
00735:                 code="MISSING_RESTAURANT_ID",
00736:             )
00737: 
00738:         if meal_id <= 0:
00739:             raise AppError(
00740:                 "شناسه وعده غذایی معتبر نیست.",
00741:                 code="INVALID_MEAL_ID",
00742:             )
00743: 
00744:         return context.model_copy(
00745:             update={
00746:                 "restaurant_id": restaurant_id,
00747:                 "meal_id": meal_id,
00748:             }
00749:         )
00750: 
00751:     @staticmethod
00752:     def _validate_payload(
00753:         action_type: str,
00754:         payload: dict[str, Any],
00755:     ) -> None:
00756:         required: dict[
00757:             str,
00758:             tuple[str, ...],
00759:         ] = {
00760:             "RESERVE_FOOD": (
00761:                 "planDetailId",
00762:             ),
00763:             "CANCEL_FOOD": (
00764:                 "planDetailId",
00765:             ),
00766:             "RATE_FOOD": (
00767:                 "reserveId",
00768:                 "rate",
00769:             ),
00770:             "DELETE_LEAVE": (
00771:                 "requestID",
00772:                 "leaveKey",
00773:             ),
00774:             "CREATE_TIME_EVENT": (
00775:                 "eventDate",
00776:                 "eventTime",
00777:             ),
00778:             "CREATE_LEAVE": (
00779:                 "absenceTypeCode",
00780:                 "startDate",
00781:             ),
00782:         }
00783: 
00784:         fields = required.get(
00785:             action_type
00786:         )
00787: 
00788:         if fields is None:
00789:             raise AppError(
00790:                 "نوع عملیات پشتیبانی نمی‌شود.",
00791:                 code="UNSUPPORTED_ACTION",
00792:                 details={
00793:                     "actionType": action_type,
00794:                 },
00795:             )
00796: 
00797:         missing = [
00798:             field
00799:             for field in fields
00800:             if payload.get(field) in (
00801:                 None,
00802:                 "",
00803:             )
00804:         ]
00805: 
00806:         if missing:
00807:             raise AppError(
00808:                 "اطلاعات عملیات کامل نیست.",
00809:                 code="INVALID_ACTION_PAYLOAD",
00810:                 details={
00811:                     "missing": missing,
00812:                 },
00813:             )

00041: 
00042: def _copy_with_update(value, changes):
00043:     model_copy = getattr(
00044:         value,
00045:         "model_copy",
00046:         None,
00047:     )
00048: 
00049:     if callable(model_copy):
00050:         return model_copy(
00051:             update=changes
00052:         )
00053: 
00054:     copy_method = getattr(
00055:         value,
00056:         "copy",
00057:         None,
00058:     )
00059: 
00060:     if callable(copy_method):
00061:         try:
00062:             return copy_method(
00063:                 update=changes
00064:             )
00065:         except TypeError:
00066:             pass
00067: 
00068:     if is_dataclass(value):
00069:         return replace(
00070:             value,
00071:             **changes,
00072:         )
00073: 
00074:     for key, item in changes.items():
00075:         setattr(
00076:             value,
00077:             key,
00078:             item,
00079:         )
00080: 
00081:     return value

00085:     try:
00086:         config = json.loads(
00087:             _PILOT_HR_OVERRIDE_PATH.read_text(
00088:                 encoding="utf-8"
00089:             )
00090:         )
00091:     except FileNotFoundError:
00092:         return context
00093:     except Exception:
00094:         logger.exception(
00095:             "PILOT_HR_OVERRIDE_INVALID_FILE"
00096:         )
00097:         return context
00098: 
00099:     if config.get("enabled") is not True:
00100:         return context
00101: 
00102:     configured_employee_id = str(
00103:         config.get(
00104:             "employeeId",
00105:             "",
00106:         )
00107:     ).strip()
00108: 
00109:     current_employee_id = str(
00110:         context.employee_id or ""
00111:     ).strip()
00112: 
00113:     if (
00114:         not configured_employee_id
00115:         or configured_employee_id
00116:         != current_employee_id
00117:     ):
00118:         return context
00119: 
00120:     # PILOT_HR_OVERRIDE_SCOPE_V1
00121:     # Only the legacy .NET pilot bridge uses the fixed
00122:     # credentials stored in pilot_hr_override.json.
00123:     # Direct Swagger calls keep the session sent in Request.
00124:     conversation_id = str(
00125:         context.conversation_id or ""
00126:     ).strip()
00127:

00132:                 "employee_id=%s "
00133:                 "reason=non_bridge_conversation"
00134:             ),
00135:             current_employee_id,
00136:         )
00137:         return context
00138:

00140:         config.get(
00141:             "authorizationToken",
00142:             "",

00147:         config.get(

00149:             "",
00150:         )

00154:         config.get(
00155:             "passwordHash",
00156:             "",
00157:         )
00158:     ).strip()
00159:
