00161:         "digitCode"
00162:     )
00163: 
00164:     if not all(
00165:         (
00166:             authorization_token,
00167:             username_hash,
00168:             password_hash,
00169:             digit_code is not None,
00170:         )

00172:         logger.error(
00173:             (
00174:                 "PILOT_HR_OVERRIDE_SKIPPED "
00175:                 "employee_id=%s reason=incomplete_config"
00176:             ),
00177:             current_employee_id,
00178:         )
00179:         return context
00180: 
00181:     session = context.session
00182: 
00183:     if session is None:
00184:         logger.error(
00185:             (
00186:                 "PILOT_HR_OVERRIDE_SKIPPED "
00187:                 "employee_id=%s reason=no_session"
00188:             ),
00189:             current_employee_id,
00190:         )
00191:         return context
00192: 
00193:     updated_session = _copy_with_update(
00194:         session,
00195:         {
00196:             "authorization_token": authorization_token,
00197:             "username_hash": username_hash,
00198:             "password_hash": password_hash,

00201:     )

00213:             "employee_id=%s"

00215:         current_employee_id,

00226: 
00227: def _fallback_conversation_id(
00228:     context: AgentContext,
00229:     identity: GatewayIdentity,
00230: ) -> str | None:
00231:     """
00232:     Build a stable conversation id for legacy callers that do not
00233:     send conversationId (for example the current .NET bridge).
00234: 
00235:     The raw token is never stored. Only a short SHA-256 digest is
00236:     used, so two requests from the same login session share state
00237:     while different sessions stay isolated.
00238:     """
00239:     employee_id = str(context.employee_id or "").strip()
00240:     if employee_id == "05000602":
00241:         return "pilot-05000602"
00242: 
00243:     token = str(
00244:         identity.authorization_token or ""
00245:     ).strip()
00246: 
00247:     if (
00248:         not token
00249:         and context.session is not None
00250:         and context.session.authorization_token is not None
00251:     ):
00252:         token = (
00253:             context.session.authorization_token
00254:             .get_secret_value()
00255:             .strip()
00256:         )
00257: 
00258:     if token.lower().startswith("bearer "):
00259:         token = token[7:].strip()
00260: 
00261:     if token:
00262:         digest = hashlib.sha256(
00263:             token.encode("utf-8")
00264:         ).hexdigest()[:32]
00265:         return f"bridge-{digest}"
00266: 
00267:     employee_id = str(
00268:         context.employee_id or ""
00269:     ).strip()
00270: 
00271:     if employee_id:
00272:         return f"employee-{employee_id}"
00273: 
00274:     return None

00430:         context
00431:     )

00434:         context
00435:     )
00436: 
00437:     return ok(
00438:         data,
00439:         (
00440:             "وضعیت یادآوری‌های روزانه بررسی شد."
00441:         ),

00483: @router.post(
00484:     "/chat",
00485:     response_model=ApiResponse[dict[str, Any]],
00486: )
00487: async def chat(
00488:     request: ChatRequest,
00489:     identity: GatewayIdentity = Depends(
00490:         require_user_identity
00491:     ),
00492: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
00493:     container = get_container()
00494: 
00495:     if request.context is not None:
00496:         raw_context = request.context.to_context()
00497:     else:
00498:         raw_context = AgentContext()
00499: 
00500:     if (
00501:         not raw_context.conversation_id
00502:         and request.conversation_id
00503:     ):
00504:         raw_context.conversation_id = (
00505:             request.conversation_id
00506:         )
00507: 
00508:     context = container.context_resolver.resolve(
00509:         raw_context,
00510:         identity,
00511:     )
00512: 
00513:     container.context_resolver.require_employee(
00514:         context
00515:     )
00516: 
00517:     if not context.conversation_id:
00518:         context.conversation_id = (
00519:             _fallback_conversation_id(
00520:                 context,
00521:                 identity,
00522:             )
00523:         )
00524: 
00525:     context = _apply_pilot_hr_override(
00526:         context
00527:     )
00528: 
00529:     session = context.session
00530: 
00531:     logger.info(
00532:         (
00533:             "CHAT_TRACE_START employee_id=%s "
00534:             "conversation_id=%s message_length=%s "
00535:             "has_token=%s has_username_hash=%s "
00536:             "has_password_hash=%s has_digit_code=%s"
00537:         ),
00538:         context.employee_id,
00539:         context.conversation_id,
00540:         len(request.message or ""),
00541:         bool(session and session.authorization_token),
00542:         bool(session and session.username_hash),
00543:         bool(session and session.password_hash),
00544:         bool(session and session.digit_code),
00545:     )
00546: 
00547:     try:
00548:         data = await container.chat_service.handle(
00549:             context,
00550:             request.message,
00551:         )
00552:     except IntegrationError as exc:
00553:         details = getattr(
00554:             exc,
00555:             "details",
00556:             None,
00557:         ) or {}
00558: 
00559:         downstream_path = str(
00560:             details.get(
00561:                 "path",
00562:                 "",
00563:             )
00564:         )
00565: 
00566:         downstream_status = details.get(
00567:             "downstreamStatusCode"
00568:         )
00569: 
00570:         if (
00571:             downstream_path.endswith(
00572:                 "/PostTimeEventSet"
00573:             )
00574:             and downstream_status == 400
00575:         ):
00576:             # HR_BUSINESS_MESSAGE_FUTURE_ATTENDANCE
00577:             logger.warning(
00578:                 (
00579:                     "HR_BUSINESS_MESSAGE_FUTURE_ATTENDANCE "
00580:                     "employee_id=%s conversation_id=%s"
00581:                 ),
00582:                 context.employee_id,
00583:                 context.conversation_id,
00584:             )
00585: 
00586:             data = {
00587:                 "reply": (
00588:                     "ثبت تردد انجام نشد. "
00589:                     "امکان ثبت تردد برای روزهای آینده وجود ندارد. "
00590:                     "لطفاً تاریخ امروز یا یکی از روزهای گذشته را وارد کنید."
00591:                 ),
00592:                 "requiresConfirmation": False,
00593:                 "pendingAction": None,
00594:                 "data": None,
00595:             }
00596:         else:
00597:             logger.exception(
00598:                 (
00599:                     "CHAT_TRACE_FAILURE employee_id=%s "
00600:                     "conversation_id=%s exception_type=%s "
00601:                     "code=%s status_code=%s details=%r"
00602:                 ),
00603:                 context.employee_id,
00604:                 context.conversation_id,
00605:                 type(exc).__name__,
00606:                 getattr(
00607:                     exc,
00608:                     "code",
00609:                     None,
00610:                 ),
00611:                 getattr(
00612:                     exc,
00613:                     "status_code",
00614:                     None,
00615:                 ),
00616:                 details,
00617:             )
00618:             raise
00619:     except Exception as exc:
00620:         logger.exception(
00621:             (
00622:                 "CHAT_TRACE_FAILURE employee_id=%s "
00623:                 "conversation_id=%s exception_type=%s "
00624:                 "code=%s status_code=%s details=%r"
00625:             ),
00626:             context.employee_id,
00627:             context.conversation_id,
00628:             type(exc).__name__,
00629:             getattr(exc, "code", None),
00630:             getattr(exc, "status_code", None),
00631:             getattr(exc, "details", None),
00632:         )
00633:         raise
00634: 
00635:     logger.info(
00636:         (
00637:             "CHAT_TRACE_SUCCESS employee_id=%s "
00638:             "conversation_id=%s keys=%s "
00639:             "requires_confirmation=%s "
00640:             "pending_action_present=%s"
00641:         ),
00642:         context.employee_id,
00643:         context.conversation_id,
00644:         sorted(data.keys()) if isinstance(data, dict) else [],
00645:         (
00646:             data.get("requiresConfirmation")
00647:             if isinstance(data, dict)
00648:             else None
00649:         ),
00650:         (
00651:             bool(data.get("pendingAction"))
00652:             if isinstance(data, dict)
00653:             else False
00654:         ),
00655:     )
00656: 
00657:     # Compatibility shim for the current .NET AgentData contract.
00658:     if (
00659:         isinstance(data, dict)
00660:         and data.get("data") is not None
00661:     ):
00662:         data = dict(data)
00663:         data["data"] = None
00664: 
00665:     return ok(
00666:         data,
00667:         "پاسخ Agent آماده شد.",
00668:     )
00669:
